- SEC-044 (a write on my own path that carries another workspace's id is refused): test_a_write_on_my_own_path_that_carries_an_id_of_another_workspace_is_refused in apps/api/tests/test_tenant_isolation_writes.py runs two mixes over WRITE_ROUTES. The body mix sends workspace B's own path with the body or query built from workspace A and ran for 16 operations. The tail mix keeps B's first path id and takes the rest of the path from A and ran for 66. All 82 got a 4xx and no row of either workspace changed. 68 answers were 404, 9 were 400, 3 were 403 and 2 were 409. Four body mixes were refused by the seeded state before the foreign id was read (adding a run to a signed-off slide, pairing an upload that is a slide, sending a report for a cosign in the wrong state, reassigning to the same reviewer), so they prove nothing about the id. Entries that skip: no path id of its own, a body that holds no id, and the six writes that are accepted with a reason. Filed TASK-016 (adding a run to a signed-off annotate slide can carry its reason) for the route that asks for a reason it cannot take.
- SEC-043 (every write route is sent as another workspace): apps/api/tests/test_tenant_isolation_writes.py reads all 314 operations from the OpenAPI schema and fails when one is in neither GET_ROUTES, WRITE_ROUTES, UNAIMED_WRITES nor PROBED_ELSEWHERE. Of the 177 write operations 146 are probed: a member of workspace B sends a path and body built from workspace A's ids and must get one exact status, and a digest of every row of A must not change. 131 get 404, 7 get 403 (the six platform routes and switching workspace), 2 get 400 (a display preset for a task you do not review, a lab model for a project of another workspace) and 6 are accepted with a written reason (two template previews that draw sample data, two template writes that keep a block's id as text, marking read and unmuting, which skip rows that are not the caller's). The owner then sends the identical request: 91 get a 2xx, 46 get the one status their entry declares from the seeded state (33 times 409, 9 times 400, 3 times 403, once 401) and 9 have no owner half (platform routes, previews and the template offer). 28 operations take no id, which a test reads from the request schema, and go unprobed with a reason. Ending a session and the two join routes need a browser session, so PROBED_ELSEWHERE names the tests in test_sessions.py and test_workspace_access.py that send them as a stranger and a test checks those exist. No write crossed a workspace. The owner half found one bug outside isolation, PROJ-012 (removing a class that stored work uses), fixed in its own commit. The file runs in about 75 seconds locally. Two gaps are left as they are: addRunToTaskSlide takes no reason, so on a signed-off slide its owner gets 400 reason-needed, and the 46 declared owner statuses prove the row was found but not that the write itself works.
- PROJ-012 (removing a class that stored work uses is refused, not a crash): PUT /api/projects/{id}/classes read only task annotations before deleting a class, so removing a class that a person's own annotation, an imported result set, a review edit, a skip or a decision points at broke a foreign key and answered 500. The owner half of the write isolation suite of SEC-043 (every write route is sent as another workspace) hit it on the seeded result set. refuse_used in parfocal_api.taxonomy now reads CLASS_USES, every column with a foreign key to project_classes.id that does not cascade, found from the table metadata, and answers 409 class-in-use. The title now names annotations, results and decisions. Built as the obvious behaviour: the class stays, nothing is nulled or deleted. test_removing_a_class_that_a_persons_own_annotation_uses_is_refused failed with the foreign key error before the fix, and test_every_column_that_keeps_a_class_alive_is_checked_before_a_class_is_removed pins the eight columns, so a new table that points at a class fails it until someone decides.

- SEC-037 (caps on room sockets and limits on the room routes): a room now refuses the ninth socket of one person with 429 room-full, checked before the socket pair is made. limitClassOf puts /rt/ in the class room (binding ROOM_LIMIT, 300 a minute per address) and /rt-internal/ in room-internal (ROOM_INTERNAL_LIMIT, 3,000 a minute), both added to apps/edge/wrangler.jsonc. handleInternal checks the shape of the signature header before it touches the body and reads at most 64 KiB, answering 413 publish-too-large past that, with or without a Content-Length. Four new tests failed first. Edge suite 97 tests, 95 pass, 2 skipped. Not done here: a rate cap on frames of an open socket and the automatic ping reply, which the finding named from memory and which needs workerd to test. The limits are guesses to tune from pilot traffic, and a person with nine tabs on one slide gets no live updates in the ninth. Production gets this at the next manual edge deploy, where the two new bindings are created by the deploy itself.
- SEC-036 (a removed person cannot rejoin a room): the user room in apps/edge/src/room.ts now records each revoke for five minutes and refuses a join or renewal with a token issued at or before it. handleConnect waits for the index write and answers 403 room-access-revoked, a refused renewal closes with 4403, and a failed index write refuses the join. The index is keyed by project and room and keeps the latest expiry, so an older token joining later cannot hide a newer socket. The web client asks the API for a fresh token on every reconnect, so only a replayed token meets the refusal. Eight new tests in rooms.test.ts, five of which failed before the fix, and one that keeps the edge's 300 seconds equal to the API's ROOM_TOKEN_SECONDS. The unused execution context left the Worker's handlers. Edge suite 93 tests, 91 pass, 2 skipped. ADR 0140 (realtime rooms and room tokens) has an update with the one gap left, a revoke that lands between the index write and the socket being accepted. No workflow deploys the edge Worker, so production gets this at the next manual deploy.

- TEST-031 (web specs at their time limit get room for a slow runner): ci run 42714305875 on commit 71294ded failed on undo.spec.ts:47, workspaces.spec.ts:64 and guided-review.spec.ts:154 while golden images, engine browser, db, ui and e2e passed. The web jobs took 33 and 34 minutes against 23 and 25, and the batch touched no file under apps/web/src, so it is a time limit and not a regression. The slide now gets 25 seconds to be ready, undo has a 60 second and workspaces a 90 second test limit, both undo reloads wait for the slide, and the six page loads of the guided review test are three tests. Locally on port 5195 with one worker and no retries 47 of 48 passed at load averages of 25 to 46 from the other session, and the one failure (the withdrawn scene, untouched) passed alone.
- VIEW-066 (settle three viewer engine suspicions): suspicions 2 and 3 of the viewer engine review were real and are fixed with tests written first. An OME-Zarr level with no declared scale now gets a downsample snapped to a power of two, and axes that list x before y are refused. A shared patch read has its own abort signal and stops only when every caller aborted. Suspicion 4 (no reader for NDPI, Ventana BIF, MRXS and DICOM) cannot fail silently, because the registry refuses each id and the viewer shows a message. Result in docs/results/2026-11-10-viewer-engine-suspicions.md.
- SEC-041 (the closed range check does not trust the size in the token): the edge Worker now turns a suffix or open range of the original into the explicit range it checked against the closed ranges, and reads exactly those bytes. A stored object or a cached entry of another size than the token was signed for answers 409 size-mismatch, and a negative size is malformed. Suffix and open ranges of the original now share a cache entry. Uploads and samples sign the stored size, so the two agree by construction. No workflow deploys the edge Worker, so production gets this at the next manual deploy. Edge suite 83 pass, 2 skipped.
- VIEW-064 (failed tiles on multichannel slides): the channel route had no failed tile map and reported an idle load state, so a failed fluorescence tile stayed blank with no placeholder, retry or slow-load hint. The map moved to viv/failed-tiles.ts and both routes use it. A channel tile stays listed until every failed channel has been read again, the channel levels report loadState, failures and clearFailure, and a disposed decode pool answers with Viv's abort sentinel. Five new unit tests in viv-channel-levels.test.ts.
- AISEG-020 (the outline worker is replaced after it stops): createAisOutliner kept a dead worker after an error, so every later outline request waited for ever, and it had no messageerror listener. It now takes an injectable spawn, drops and terminates the worker on error or messageerror, rejects the waiting requests and spawns a fresh worker for the next request. A late failure of a replaced worker is ignored. Five unit tests in apps/web/test/aiseg-ais-outliner.test.ts, the first of which failed before the fix.

## 2026-11-09

- TEST-029 (display panel specs click the Undo of the toast they caused): two display panel tests flaked in three ci runs because they waited for the 4 second undo toast and then clicked a bare Undo, which was detached on a slow runner. They now click the Undo inside the toast that names the preset. Both pass locally with one worker and no retries. In review until three ci runs show no flaky record. TEST-030 (find why the table Tab order test fails on its first ci attempt): not reproduced on the Mac in 10 plain runs, with the CPU slowed 6 and 20 times, or with the story script delayed 400 ms. The test now reports every Tab stop it reached, so the next ci failure names the element. Left open. The worklist-live test and the auto-region long task budget each flaked once, in runs older than the commits that steadied them (54be52fe and 663ab37f), so they got no change. Recorded in knowledge/flaky-tests.md.
- Browser runs for the review batch on the Mac, with load averages near 30 from another session's runs: engine browser specs 140 passed and 1 failed, golden images 82 passed and 1 failed with 18 not run behind it. The engine failure is the wheel zoom budget on Huron-1-40x.tif (68 ms against a limit of 50), and unchanged main at c68955bc measured 70 ms in the same minute, so it is the load. The golden failure is the ID labels layer, whose golden comes from the Linux CI runner, and unchanged main differs by the same 2,148 pixels on the Mac. The 23 other overlay goldens pass on the branch. VIEW-059 (black edge row) stays unticked until ci is green on the pushed head.
- VIEW-068 (an inference worker that stops is an error, not a hang): createSamClient listened only for message, so a worker that failed to load or crashed left ready and every request pending. It now uses onWorkerFailure and rejects ready, the waiting requests and every later one, with three unit tests that hung before the fix. This corrects the VIEW-060 (a decode worker that fails to start) note, which said the other worker clients were not checked: the triangulation worker already listens for errors, the inference client is fixed here, and the outline worker in apps/web keeps a dead worker after an error, filed as AISEG-020 (the outline worker is replaced after it stops).
- FOUND-038 (records where an agent stood in for a person): docs/project/agent-stand-in-records.md lists the pathologist wording review in ADR 0194 (confidence display rules), the tabletop record with ADR 0186 (incident response), the seven compliance pages and the threat model that no counsel or security professional read, the four agent reviews of 2026-11-09, the measurements that use rule-placed clicks or a reviewer who never errs, and the records decided for the owner. Each listed file carries a Simulated check line, except result files, which already describe their method and are not edited after the fact. TODO in the page: no one-page summary exists for the records after ADR 0091.
- FOUND-037 (docs memory that cannot go stale): board.py counts prints tasks and ticks in total, per milestone gate (the epics the gate names) and per epic, with a test, and the milestone table now parses MA (the annotation path). The docs were consolidated the same day: README In progress, Where we are now and Next up rewritten from the board with no hand-written count, the roadmap got its MA row and every milestone is In progress, risks R6, R11 and R15 were rewritten and R18 (fake API), R19 (agent permissions) and R20 (agent stand-ins) added, six result files missing from the results index were added and one duplicate row removed. agent-workflow.md makes the lead consolidate at the end of every parallel run.
- SEC-038 (tile and room keys of at least 32 bytes): the room route signed tokens with a key such as change-me, and the tile route with an empty one. token_keys.usable_secret in the API now counts a key that is empty, not base64 or under 32 bytes as missing, so tile access and room access answer 503 and realtime publishing turns itself off. The Worker's signingKey raises WeakSigningKeyError for the same keys and the Worker answers 503 signing-key-not-configured. Seven API tests failed before the fix. The local .dev.vars keys decode to 48 bytes, the CI and test keys to 32 or more. Production keys are not set by any workflow yet, so the first deploy must use 32 random bytes as ADR 0137 (capability tokens for slide bytes) says.
- SEC-039 (security headers after a throw): a room path that cannot be decoded is now a 404 room-not-found problem, and the Worker catches any throw or rejection from the limits guard and the router, logs the error name and message, and answers a 500 internal-error problem, so every reply passes through withSecurityHeaders. Two edge tests failed before the fix.
- VIEW-061 (no decode workers left behind): showSlide spawned the workers and then built the levels, which throw for a slide the RGB route cannot draw, so each attempt leaked six workers. showSlide now builds through disposingOnThrow, which disposes the reader when the build throws. The unit test drives that helper with a real reader, a counting worker factory and the real createVivLevels on a 16-bit grey source. The route itself needs WebGL and has no Node test, so its one call of the helper is covered only for slides that build, by the browser specs.
- VIEW-060 (a decode worker that fails to start): the worker reader and the patch worker client listened only for message, so a worker script that failed to load, or a message that could not be read, left ready and every read pending. Both now listen for error and messageerror, reject ready and every read in flight, refuse later reads, and the reader reports once through onFailure, which the Viv route sends to its error handler. A failed worker fails the whole reader instead of one slot, because a slot that rejects at once would take every next job. The page also tells each worker how many levels it has, and a worker that opened fewer, which happens when its overview levels file fails once, now fails its open. Seven unit tests failed before the fix. Not done: the SAM and triangulation worker clients were not checked for the same gap.
- VIEW-067 (a range far past the file cannot freeze the byte cache): found while reproducing VIEW-063. The range cache walked every block of a range before it knew the file size, so a corrupt offset froze the page or the worker. A range that spans more blocks than the cache holds now goes straight to the network. The unit test hung before the fix.
- VIEW-063 (sharded OME-Zarr through the byte cache): zarrita read a shard index with a HEAD and then a range. The byte cache and the worker relay forward only the URL and the Range header, so the HEAD became a GET of the whole shard with no length, the index was read from the wrong bytes, and the tile read froze the thread. openOmeZarrSource now opens FetchStore with useSuffixRequest, which sends one bytes=-N range. The new test hung before the fix and passes after it. The same viewport now costs 8 requests where it cost 12, recorded in the zarr range coalescing result. The edge gateway already answers a suffix range with 206, and the test server learned suffix ranges.
- VIEW-065 (short 206 reply): the range cache stored a 206 reply with fewer bytes than asked for as a whole block and padded it with zeros. fetchRun now compares the reply with the bytes asked for, clipped to the file size, and rejects. A unit test shows the read rejecting and no block cached.
- VIEW-059 (black edge row on a short backing level): both downsample loops in viv-levels.ts and viv-channel-levels.ts read past the stored size when a virtual level's backing level is shorter than width divided by the residual, which left a zero row or column at the slide edge, and a tile column past the stored width asked for an empty window. Both now repeat the last stored sample, and backingWindow clamps the plan to at least one pixel. Five unit tests failed before the fix. Indexed opens were affected too, because ingest writes the stored sizes to levels.json.
- SEC-033 (isolation checks that read the policies and probe every table): test_the_policies_at_head_are_the_reviewed_ones pins all 107 policies in apps/api/tests/fixtures/policies_at_head.json, 75 standard and 32 others in full, and four mutation tests show a loosened or added policy differing. A new probe reads every table with a tenant_id column as parfocal_app under the neighbour's scope, a bare user scope and no scope, and fails when the suite seeds no row. A manual mutation (USING (true) on cases) made it fail. The write routes moved to SEC-043 (every write route is sent as another workspace). The role check of SEC-032 now also refuses a member of a bypassing role, which is how Neon's console roles are made.
- Filed the viewer engine review as eight VIEW tasks (one per finding, the black edge row on virtual levels first) and the edge token review as seven SEC tasks, with decision 85 (presence in a blinded review). Both result files are in docs/results. FOUND-033 (a result for every push to main) stays in review: a queued run on main is still replaced by a newer push.

After the Fable review a workspace may claim only its admin's own verified email domain, a save without a logo keeps it, and another workspace's admin gets 404 on decline. Image logo upload needs a migration and storage, left for later. Decision 79 asks about shared domains.
- Added the Kind step and annotate path to the new task stepper with presets, optional result sets, check options and a disabled blind first switch until COLLAB-017, with model tests and E2E from the project page and after an upload. Fable review passed with 27 browser tests. Follow-up TASK-018.
- COLLAB-015 Correction from the review, the draft GET /api/slides/{slide_id}/threads route is not in main's openapi or code, so the project-scoped path is a plain choice, not forced by a clash.
- Migration 0105 makes threads.task_id nullable, a project-scoped slide thread route shares code with the task route, slide threads follow the first-read rule, and the pin layer and Comments tab show them. Fable review passed with 1067 permission matrix cases and found the migration safe for production. Follow-ups are COLLAB-018 and decision 78.
- Fake result set detail and cell summary answer 403 ai-hidden, and RT-17 objects stay empty until the first read. Guided review treats only a 404 from getFirstRead as not recorded and shows a retry panel with the AI hidden on other errors. Blinding the slide and project result set lists moves to TASK-017 because it breaks the A1 specs.
- Guided check key safety: key actions are ignored while a save runs, Shift G works only with the AI shown on an unlocked slide, and row clicks keep the guided selection, with unit and browser tests.
- An ADR confirms PostHog Cloud for STACK-033 and records the ids-only analytics event taxonomy. EXP-001 still needs the production client, the identity hook, the exposure event and the workspace as a PostHog group.
- An ADR confirms STACK-012 (frontend testing stack) and gives six test levels an owning place and CI job each, with file counts measured by git ls-files.
- An ADR lists input device support and fallbacks for mouse, trackpad, touch, pen, keyboard, footswitch, gamepad and SpaceMouse over WebHID. Browser support and the SpaceMouse report layout are marked TODO to confirm in DEV-004 with recorded reports.
- SHELL-010 Correction, the review follow-up is SHELL-015 (one connection banner and issue pages everywhere), not SHELL-016.
- TASK-010 Correction, the review follow-ups are TASK-016 (mock API blinds like the real one) and TASK-017 (first read notice in the full viewer and paged screen), not TASK-019 and TASK-020.
- Guided check follows the task's stored review order and scope with the client sort as fallback, covered by unit tests and a scoped browser spec. Objects a model run adds wait for the order to be recomputed.
- Shell issue pages for not found, server error, session expired and no access, and a real Connection lost banner with Retry now and a restored toast, with 6 browser tests and axe. Fable review asked for one fix, retries now run one at a time with a timeout. Wiring into the review and viewer flows is SHELL-016.
- An ADR records the linked compare pane model, a per-pane offset in slide pixels, log2 zoom and degrees, relink keeping the manual offset and rigid automatic alignment. Saving an aligned offset per slide pair is decision 77.
- An ADR records the settings schema with every catalogue key, its scope and default, the stores outside the catalogue and the planned device keys. Notification rows stay in the preferences table.
- Guided review in a hide-the-AI task opens a first-read step with the AI hidden, records it through submitFirstRead and then reveals the AI, with an E2E showing the first read unchanged after decisions and submit. Fable review asked for one fix, Record my first read now waits for the last answer and added object to save. Follow-ups TASK-019 and TASK-020.
- Confirmed STACK-024 (realtime transport) and recorded the event model as an ADR, ids and kinds posted to project, slide, task and user rooms scoped by tenant. The API to edge hop stays unmeasured until a staging API.
- VIZ-000 Correction, the mounting follow-up is VIZ-010 (mount the engine overlay draws on the live slide), not VIZ-020. VIZ-007 (Display panel) should read getMyDisplay (ADR 0233) instead of the draft getTaskDisplay.
- An ADR records how result sets, styles and maps draw on the overlay system and how Display state and locks reach it. The live slide still draws SVG stills, so VIZ-020 now mounts the engine draws.
- An ADR records the sign-up and setup flow as built on IAM-002 and IAM-007, with an invite winning over a domain match and a domain match over creating a workspace, and names the draft auth calls and ONB-011 as what is left.
- GUIDE-019 Follow-ups added from the Fable review: GUIDE-020 (guided check follows the task's check scope and order) and GUIDE-021 (guided check key safety).
- ADMIN-000 Correction, the follow-up for the mock controls is ADMIN-010 (wire the class lists, retention and session lock controls), not ADMIN-016.
- ADR 0266 lists the ten admin sections with their permissions and build state. Class lists, retention and session lock are still mock controls, and ADMIN-016 now wires them.
- Guided check in Annotate: Shift G walks unchecked AI objects least sure first, with confirm, re-class, delete and skip on keys and the queue growing with new objects. Fable review asked for one fix, the fix key now does nothing on a one-class task, and named follow-ups GUIDE-020 and GUIDE-021.
- Case creation UI completed with Upload slides on each case row for owners opening the case upload drawer, and a browser spec that creates a case in a de-identified and a clinical project and uploads a slide into each.
- Found the web browser suite slows by a steady runner-dependent factor of up to 1.9 with no build-up over a run, and split browser (web) into three Playwright shards. Result in docs/results/2026-11-09-web-browser-suite-ci-slowdown.md.
- Search text moved out of the URL: kept in page state, the palette hands it over in router state, a reload starts empty, and the search spec has no q parameter.
- VIEW-031 Ticked, ci run on main at 5d16c289 is green including the wheel zoom budget, and the owner chose 74a.
- HOME-012 Added from decision 72a on 2026-11-09, search text moves out of the URL.
- ONB-010 Owner chose 75a on 2026-11-09, join at once by default stays as built.
- ONB-004 Owner chose 73a on 2026-11-09, self-service creation with one workspace per person stays as built.
- VIEW-031 Owner chose 74a on 2026-11-09, main-thread task time is the CI check and frame time stays a manual budget. Still waits only for a green hosted CI browser run.
- A fourth red ci run on main (42571630780) failed lint on a 504 while downloading a tool, not on code, and issue-states part 4 on its 60 second limit, naming whichever scene was loading, signin noaccess then settings reserved. No scene broke. Every issue-states part grew from about 32 to 56 seconds over four runs, and unrelated specs slowed as much, so the issue-states and full-viewer parts now get 120 seconds and the comment pin measurement spec 60, with assertions unchanged. TEST-021 (find why the web browser suite slowed on CI) is added for the cause.
- A third red ci run on main (42558001432) had four causes. The prototype index count was out of date after VIZ-005 (swipe and side by side) added the sidebyside scene. The slide's outline choice was lost on reload after SET-005 (AI and confidence section), because that control still wrote only local storage while the saved setting won on load, so it now saves the personal setting. The side panel width and hidden state were lost after SET-004 (Layout section) made saving an async request, and quick arrow presses read a stale width, so changes now build on the latest layout and the spec waits for each save. The invert zoom and Esc drag specs load the viewer twice and now get 60 seconds, as web suite times on hosted runners ranged from 38 to 66 minutes. The overlay holes golden and the four viewers memory spec in the viewer engine did not reproduce locally and look like a lost WebGL context on the runner, with goldens and assertions unchanged. Each settings task had run only the specs it touched, which is how these reached main.
- A second red ci run on main (42554055455) failed a two-factor settings test from ADMIN-009 (named actors in Admin) that reached Postgres without the db marker, the every-kind notification target test from CASES-021 (back targets in deep links) after ONB-010 (join or ask for a workspace) added an access request kind with no screen to open, and the thread reply target expectation that lacked the new origin, case_id and resolved_from fields. The test is now marked db, the access request kind is checked to answer 409 target-not-available beside integration down, and the exact match carries the new fields. The tenant isolation suite was fixed by AIP-014 (AI usage metering). The lead's merge check does not run database tests, which let these reach main.
- AIP-014 (AI cost and usage metering) adds report_gpu_seconds, which a model-running step calls with the GPU seconds it measured, and migration 0104 adds a gpu_usage table under forced row-level security with no UPDATE for the app role, labelled with tenant, job, job kind, model, version, tier, GPU type and worker, written at once so paused or failed runs still count. GET /api/tenant/ai-usage returns total seconds and jobs, seconds per model and version and the 200 latest jobs, gated on the workspace-settings permission so admins and researchers can read it and reviewers get 403, in seconds only because no GPU price is recorded. No real model worker calls it yet. It also adds listDeletedProjects from PROJ-011 to the tenant isolation suite, which had been failing on main.
- TEST-013 (workflow and job tests) adds eight database restart tests for the Postgres job runner, ingest and the pre-read model run, with the lease clock moved in SQL and worker deaths simulated as steps that throw partway. Ingest survives a death while deriving and after publishing and fails for good after three claims, and a pre-read whose GPU worker dies after two tiles writes the same chunks as a clean run. It fixed a real bug in jobs.py, where a worker that froze and lost its lease could wake up and still mark its step done and record throughput against the worker that took over, by checking ownership under a row lock in the same transaction. A Fable review found nothing to fix, and noted that the step-start write has no ownership check yet, a rare case where a stale worker can bump the taker's run count.
- ADMIN-005 (Admin AI models) found the Parfocal models with the Shown switch and undo, the lab's models with status tags and a Running now list built, where Cancel only hid the row and never called the API. Cancel now hides the run with an undo toast and sends the real cancel request when the 6 second undo window ends or the page is left, and a failed call puts the run back with the error. Browser tests hide a model and undo it, and cancel a run, undo, cancel again and see the request go out.
- LAB-002 (performance HUD) replaces the one-line developer text with a HUD showing fps, frame time and its p95, resident tiles, tiles in flight, decode p95, annotations drawn and culled and the LOD tier, refreshed every 250 ms, placed in a corner chosen in the developer drawer and hidden with its close button. Atlas use is built but shows only on a WebGPU route, which does not exist yet. The decode pool now tracks decode p95 and the Viv route reports resident tiles from deck.gl's tile caches, shown as a dash when they cannot be read, not yet confirmed in a browser. The HUD's own update work measured 0.0032 ms per frame over 20,000 frames in Node against the 0.5 ms limit, in docs/results/2026-11-09-performance-hud-cost.md, which leaves the browser's layout cost as an open measurement.
- VIZ-005 (swipe and side by side) makes the full viewer's swipe line a draggable slider that also moves with the arrow keys, Shift, Home and End, with set B clipped at the line over set A on one camera, and adds a Side by side view in the Display View group that shows sets A and B in two panes on a shared camera, with its own scene in the prototype inventory. Browser specs drag the swipe and check the clip after a zoom, and check that both panes keep the same view after a pan and a zoom. The side by side layout has no mock and was not checked by eye, and the minimap and magnification bar still sit in the left pane.
- ONB-003 (invite landing) found the landing with who invited, the project and task, the work, due date, blinding and privacy mode, the account step and the one-step join that opens the first slide already built, with its browser test passing. It added a clear page for an invite link that is unknown, expired or withdrawn with a Go to sign in button, where the page had stayed blank, and made the fake invite preview refuse unknown tokens, with a browser test and an api-client test.
- GUIDE-017 (pick the class blind on cells) counts a blind class answer on a cell against the AI's class read from the cell's result chunk in object storage through the existing chunk reader, for reviewer answers, owner decisions in the disagreement queue and adjudicator decisions, so the blind-cells-unsupported refusal is gone with no migration. API tests over cells in two chunks count the AI's class as agree and another as disagree, and a cell with no AI class or in no chunk as disagree. Finding a cell reads the chunks in turn, and the AI call saved with a cell decision still comes from the reviewer's client.
- ADMIN-004 (Admin Projects) found the archive API, its archived_at column, owners-only rule and API tests built, and added to the admin Projects section an Active and Archived switch, a Status column and Archive or Unarchive on each row, enabled only for the project's owners, with a notice that an archived project stays readable and can be unarchived, beside the Deleted projects zone from PROJ-011. The fake API gained archive and restore handlers, node tests cover the status labels, the owners-only rule and the notices, and a browser step archives P07, finds it under Archived and unarchives it.
- A red ci run on main (42544294851) failed three browser specs. The coverage and wheel speed specs ran into the 30 second test timeout on a hosted runner 30 to 50 percent slower than usual, and were not broken by PROJ-010 (delete a project), whose fake state resets on every page load. They now have 60 seconds, like other specs that load the viewer twice. The auto region budget test measured a 4 ms timer gap that picks up scheduling noise, so it now counts long task entries only and takes the fastest of three passes, as ADR 0154 (performance budgets) asks for time budgets, with the baseline of 34 ms and limit of 100 ms unchanged. With the CPU slowed four times it still reads 87 to 132 ms, so AISEG-019 (resample auto region tiles off the main thread) is added as the lasting fix.
- SET-006 (Navigation and devices section) found the main device, per-device speed and dead zone built, and made the mouse wheel mode, with detect device as the default, invert zoom for the wheel only, camera feel Smooth, Precise or Instant with coasting, animated jump and zoom anchor overrides, and the last two magnifications double-click choice drive the open viewer live, the last through a small viewer engine input change with the default unchanged. A Try it grid previews the chosen feel. A browser spec covers the rows, the preview, persistence after a reload and inverted wheel zoom on a real slide. The objective detents setting is saved but nothing reads it until VIEW-046 (objective detents) is built.
- SET-007 (Shortcuts section) found the CMD-006 rebinding editor with its three presets, all, changed and conflicts filters, reset all, the swap or take, reserved key and report safety prompts, the Deciding group and the question mark key opening Settings at Shortcuts already built and covered by settings.spec.ts. It added node tests for the swap or take outcomes, a browser-reserved key refused with nothing changed, and a report-changing key that needs Cmd or Ctrl.
- AILEARN-006 (held-out validation split from recent labels) adds validation_split.py, a pure function that puts each slide's training examples on the training or validation side by a hash of its tenant id and slide id, with 20 percent held out by default, so all cells of a slide stay together, a slide never changes side across rounds and the validation set grows as new slides are labelled. Ten unit tests on synthetic labels check that no slide sits on both sides, later rounds keep earlier sides, input order changes nothing and a fixed slide lands in a known bucket. Examples carry no timestamp, so there is no time window yet.
- AIP-015 (model serving health in the UI) adds a serving state, ready or offline, to the model tier list and the project model list through a WorkerProbe interface, and the API refuses to start a run on an offline model with 409 model-offline. An offline tier shows offline and cannot be picked, an offline model in the run window is disabled and blocks Run with its estimate saying it is not answering, and the admin AI models list tags it Offline. The fake API can take workers offline and bring them back, and node tests simulate an outage on all three screens and the recovery, with API tests for the state and the refusal. The default probe reports every worker ready, because no real worker health source exists yet.
- ADMIN-003 (People and roles) found the section built, with the role dropdown, the admin rights switch disabled for the last holder and absent on reviewers, projects, last active, status, invite, resend, search and the responsibilities table in the words of ADR 0096. It made the fake API record audit events for a role change, admin rights given or removed and an invite resend, where the fake audit log had been a fixed list, and added a browser test that does all four and finds them in the Audit log. The real API already audits them.
- SET-005 (AI and confidence section) makes the rest of the section drive the UI. Wording shows a live example, outline display is a saved personal setting whose API values now match the viewer's fade, mark-unsure and bands, and a personal outline model tier sets the annotate view's starting tier. decision_cutoff is a lab setting shown locked in Settings and edited by admins in a new Decision cutoff zone, and it sets the default score cutoff in the full viewer and guided review unless a task preset sets its own. The API refuses it from a reviewer's personal settings with 400 and from non-admins on workspace settings with 403. The inspector gained Your track record and Calibration details blocks with their own toggles. The lab cutoff reaching the viewer has no test yet, because the node runner cannot import the tsx file that holds it.
- ADMIN-002 (Admin Overview) found the screen already built by earlier admin work, with four stat tiles, four section summaries and the compact storage split bar from ADMIN-006, and added the browser spec it lacked, which checks the tiles, the summaries with recent activity, the storage bar and the Audit log link over seeded fake data. The screen differs from the mock on 4.6 percent of pixels, not yet looked at.
- VIZ-006 (object table and stats) found the table, counts, histograms, selection highlight and confidence-following Score column built. It made the CSV button download a real CSV with full IDs and the table's columns instead of a toast, and showed the Preset column only when a preset other than the task default is active, with that preset's name on decided rows. Presets are not stored per decision, so a decided row shows the preset active now. The row, column and CSV logic moved to object-table-rows.ts, with node tests for the Score and Preset columns and the CSV line count and browser specs for a row click selecting the object and the download. Slide-to-table selection is not tested yet.
- CASES-021 (back targets in deep links) adds an origin made only of a route name and ids to every notification target and search hit, worked out from where the target sits now, and leaves search text out because it could be a patient name. A link to a moved slide returns its current case, and a link to a merged object returns the object it was merged into with the old name in resolved_from, through the reader's own object history. Database tests open a notification of every kind and follow links after a slide move and an object merge. The integration down kind stays unavailable until REMOTE-003 (events out) builds deliveries, the web app does not read origins yet, and shared report links have no origin because their reader is not signed in.
- ADMIN-009 (named actors and real sign-in settings in Admin) shows a member's name in the audit log list and Overview's recent activity only to callers with the audit permission in the same workspace, resolved with one tenant-scoped query and only while the actor is still a member, and the export keeps pseudonyms. A two_factor_required workspace setting is saved from the Security page by admins only and audited with its new value. Nothing enforces it at sign-in yet, so the page says so, and single sign-on stays locked until IAM-012 (per-organisation SSO). A Fable review found the page claiming enforcement, the audit missing the value, a duplicate fake row and a missing name fallback, all fixed before merge.
- ONB-010 (join or ask for a workspace and keep the setup profile) replaces the draft answerWorkspace with real endpoints to list joinable workspaces, join, ask, list pending asks and approve, matched by the person's verified email domain, with public mail domains and their country variants never treated as a lab domain. Migration 0103 only adds: specialties on people, an email domain, join policy and text logo on workspaces, a join requests table under row-level security and a notification kind. Asking notifies every admin with ids only, and joining or approval makes the person a reviewer. A Fable review found audit rows pointing at the person instead of the request, a join race returning 500, missing public mail providers, stale asks after a direct join and only the first match shown in setup, all fixed before merge. There is no screen yet for admins to answer asks, decline them or set the domain and policy.
- SET-004 (Layout section) found the rail or dock choice, minimap mode and pinned strip rows built, and made the saved side panel width, hide and panel while moving settings drive the viewer panel, which had kept its own browser-local copy so Settings and the slide's Hide while panning checkbox did nothing to each other. useInspectorLayout now reads and writes the personal settings with the cache updated at once, and usePinnedStrip uses the saved slide_strip setting. A new browser spec covers width, hide, panel while moving, the strip and the minimap mode.
- TEST-011 (contract tests for the API client) adds six recorded responses from the real API test app (health, project create, list and read, a not-found problem and taxonomy templates) with ids and times fixed so they hold no PHI. A database test compares live responses with them and re-records with PARFOCAL_RECORD=1 pnpm test:db, and a verify-time node test checks each against the openapi.json schema with ajv and runs it through the generated client, including the typed ApiError. The existing OpenAPI drift test already fails CI on a stale schema.
- SET-003 (Display section) finishes the Settings Display section on top of the theme, density, lengths and account menu toggles already built. It adds patient names as full or initials only, saved per person and locked when the lab sets it, a magnification label as objective or micrometres per pixel that the slide's magnification bar follows, and English as the only language with Cestina shown disabled. Slide brightness in Settings now shares one store with the slide's Alt+Up and Alt+Down keys, so it changes the slide live. A browser spec checks each setting live and after a reload.
- VIZ-008 (how objects draw on the live slide) adds createObjectDrawing in the viewer engine, which turns the Display panel's draw, width, opacity, colour, cutoff and confidence choices into live Viv overlays through drawObjectOverlays and removes overlays that no longer apply. Nine live golden scenes match the VIZ-002 still-preview goldens for outline widths 1 to 3, fill, dots, opacity, colour by score, colour by decision and the cutoff, with no golden regenerated. The current-tinted still is not matched live, because the live tint follows the object's score. The web app's Display panel does not call it yet, because the web viewer has no live Viv overlay host.
- PROJ-011 (restore a deleted project and revoke its links) adds GET /api/projects/deleted, listing the caller's deleted projects where they own them, a Deleted projects zone with Restore in Admin Projects, and lets an owner revoke a deleted project's report links through the existing revoke route, with non-owners refused. A browser test deletes a project, finds it in Deleted projects, restores it and sees it back in Admin and on Home, and API tests cover the list and the revoke. The revoke has no screen yet, because a deleted project's task page cannot be opened.
- VIZ-009 (confidence map in the View group) adds a Confidence map entry after Cell density with a 0.40 threshold and legend and its own mock scene, and locks it with the heatmap and density map when a task locks the heatmap or hides confidence. The web viewer draws maps in SVG and never calls the engine, so instead of a pixel golden the browser test checks that the on-screen ramp uses the engine's three confidence colours. The engine's own confidence golden is unchanged.
- Parallel run of 2026-11-08 and 2026-11-09 ended at 100 tasks ticked or in review. In review: VIEW-031 (wheel zoom check, waits for decision 74 and a hosted runner run), DEV-008 (tablet layout, waits for the owner's look) and TEST-017 (flaky quarantine, waits for green CI and one manual flaky-report run). COLLAB-016 (calm live updates on a shared layer) is in progress on branch worktree-agent-a978fbc6012a707d1, whose node tests hang verify. Decisions 72 (search text in the URL), 73 (who may create a workspace) and 74 (wheel zoom as main-thread time) wait for the owner. The run added follow-up tasks for the gaps it found. ADR 0263 (lab model endpoints with sealed credentials) was missing from the index, which kept the docs workflow red since JOBS-005, and is now listed.
- PROJ-010 (delete a project) lets any project owner delete a project from the Settings danger zone after typing its name, checked on the server. It sets only the project's own deleted_at, so every row and stored file stays and POST /api/projects/{id}/undelete restores it, writes project.deleted and project.undeleted audit events with ids only, and replaces the old sample-only delete. Fable's review led to closing four leaks: a shared report link answers 410, a deleted project sends no new notifications and hides its old ones, every role and an open emergency grant lose access, and lab models no longer list it. No screen lists deleted projects for restore yet.
- PILOT-007 (feedback channel) adds in-app feedback from the account menu and command palette, with a screenshot blurred in the browser over slides, images, patient details, status and alert notices and anything marked data-phi before it is sent through POST /api/feedback, stored in object storage under feedback/{tenant}/{id}/ with an audit entry holding no text. Fable's review led to sending the matched route id instead of the URL so link tokens never leave, data-phi on every case label, slide label and file name, scroll offsets kept in the copied page, and a 413 above 6 MB before the body is read. The feedback code, the survey card and the notifications panel body now load lazily, bringing the first page to 339,048 gzip bytes. Nobody is notified yet because email routing is on hold, so feedback is read by listing that storage folder.
- EXP-005 (in-app SUS and SEQ surveys) adds a bottom-right survey card in the global shell that asks the seven-point SEQ after a finished task and the ten standard SUS items after five sessions, rate limited and dismissible, held back while a slide is open or analytics is opted out. Answers go as numbers keyed by ids through seq_answered, sus_answered and survey_dismissed with no free-text field, and the scrubbing allow-lists carry the new fields.
- The all-code gzip budget web.all-code-gzip-bytes was re-baselined from 2,461,599 to 2,948,744 bytes, the measured main build of 2026-11-09 00:50, with the limit at 3,538,000, 20 percent above as the budget rule asks. The growth since 2026-11-02 came from the features of the parallel run of 2026-11-08 in lazy chunks, while the first page stays at 345,718 bytes under its own 350,000 limit. EXP-005 (surveys) would have crossed the old limit by 5 KB.
- TEST-017 (flaky test management) quarantines a test with a @quarantine tag and an entry in flaky-quarantine.json that needs an issue link, a reason and an expiry at most 30 days out, checked by the guard tests. CI keeps one retry but lists every test that failed then passed in the job summary and as a flaky artifact for 14 days, and a Monday flaky-report workflow opens a GitHub issue with the quarantined and flaky tests. docs/knowledge/flaky-tests.md explains it.
- OBJ-004 (ID resolution and search) makes object search resolve a whole object path, any trailing part of it or a bare ID typed on a slide, with percent-encoded segments and both spellings, through new optional project_id, slide_id and task_id context on /api/search. An ID merged or split away resolves to its current object in the searching reviewer's own review layer from review_edits, and search stays in the caller's tenant and readable slides and returns no AI region, cell or zone IDs while a first read hides the AI. No migration.
- PROJ-006 (project page) found most of the page built and made the Slides tab search, result set filter and the Needs review and No results chips filter the rows through a filter model, opening matching cases and showing No slides match these filters when empty. A node test and Playwright specs cover filtering, opening a slide from a row and dropping files to start an upload. Open shows on every row rather than only on hover, to keep the touch target audit.
- Main CI fix after run 42532728025 went red: the auto region spec also asserted zero long tasks, which a hosted runner cannot meet, so it now judges the pass only by the aiseg.region-main-thread-task-ms budget, and the compare layouts spec now picks the compare grid by data-layout without data-slot, because SHELL-006 gave the tool rail a data-layout attribute too.
- EXP-012 (task timer in the review flows) starts and stops the task timer in the guided review, full viewer, zone gallery and sign-off and samples chrome coverage in the viewer, recording ids-only events after the opt-out gate in a local event log under the new storage key parfocal.event-log. The KPI dashboard reads the recorded events and falls back to the seeded sample when there are none. A browser test reviews and signs out a slide, reads the slide, area and cell times and sees nothing recorded after opt-out.
- EXP-006 (task timing for KPIs) added four ids-only events for area time, cell time, chrome coverage and reading-time accuracy, a task timer that sends them through the opt-out sink, and a lazy KPI dashboard at /kpi on seeded data judged against the kit's 15 minutes per slide and 35 percent chrome budget, with No kit target yet for the rest. Two new fields joined both scrubbing allow-lists. Nothing calls the timer from the viewer or sign-out yet, which EXP-012 (task timer in the review flows) wires.
- REVIEW-031 (unsure zones follow the camera on paged results) loads a field of unsure cells whenever the camera moves, keeps up to ten fields and the cells already decided, and builds the whole-slide zone list from a one-pass unsure index of the densest squares in each block. A browser test on 250,000 cells pans to a far zone, jumps between cells there and finds the first decision still shown. It also fixed the review screen treating every cell as being in the first field.
- WL-020 (the API announces worklist changes) publishes worklist.changed with ids only to the user rooms of everyone a change touches, after a new task, an added reviewer, a reassignment or its undo, a slide submit or reopen, a task sign-off, reopen or edit, and a finished ingest job through a new finished-job hook, and a failed publish is logged and never fails the request or job. The ingest worker announces only once ROOM_TOKEN_KEY and REALTIME_INTERNAL_URL are set, ML worker model runs and a reviewer's first edit announce nothing yet.
- WL-016 (realtime worklist updates) makes My work and Home refresh when the user realtime room announces worklist.changed, loaded lazily from those screens, and fake mode delivers it between tabs over a BroadcastChannel so a two-session E2E sees a new task within 2 s. The API does not publish worklist.changed yet, so the real room sends nothing until WL-020 (the API announces worklist changes).
- REVIEW-030 (the review screen reads paged results) connected the full viewer, the zone gallery and focus mode to the engine's paged cell reader, behind ?pagedCells=N in the fake API. A browser test on 250,000 synthetic cells generated per chunk checks the fade, a zone, a decision saved with its AI call and focus mode. The zone and jump set is the 1,200 least-sure cells in one field at the slide centre, so only the faded outlines follow the camera, which REVIEW-031 (unsure zones follow the camera on paged results) extends.
