- CI run 41743132125 on main (f3bd8446, the end of the 2026-11-03 night run) failed in three browser jobs, left for the next session. (1) golden-images: the id-labels golden from OBJ-005 (IDs on the slide) was made on the Mac and its text does not match the Linux runner, so viv-overlay-id-labels.png (and likely viv-overlay-stack.png) needs regenerating on Linux. (2) ui axe: the components-inspector-panel--sentinel-node story from CASE-014 (inspector tabs) fails scrollable-region-focusable on the side-panel body in both themes. A plain tabIndex on the div is refused by biome's noNoninteractiveTabindex, so it needs a proper focusable region or a story that does not overflow. (3) web: new-project-and-case.spec.ts from PROJ-007 failed and the Vite dev server then refused connections, so every later new-task.spec.ts test failed with ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED. Both specs pass together on the Mac, and the log shows unhandled /api/review-tasks//slides//verdicts and /order requests with empty IDs from screens/slide-progress.ts. The test and db jobs passed. Earlier the same night the commitlint check was taught to skip PR merge commits, and five review tasks were ticked from a green browser (web) job.
- INGEST-019 (slide metadata editing): additive migration 0082 adds a nullable slides.position (renumbered at merge after JOBS-006's 0081), and getCase sorts by position, then created time, then label. PATCH /api/slides/{slide_id} edits stain, block, level and label, and PUT /api/cases/{case_id}/slide-order sets the order, both for the project owner with create-cases. Changing label text in a clinical project also needs see-labels for the whole project (ADR 0124). Edits are audited with field names and counts only, never label text. Owners get an Edit slides dialog on each case header. TODO: the project slides tab still lists newest first and reads stain from the draft overview.
- AINUC-006 (calibration): workers/ml fits one temperature per typer and view count on PanNuke fold 1 and computes test-set accuracy, confident share, unsure share and validated sites (at least 100 test nuclei typed at least 70 percent right, 18 of 19 tissues pass, Lung at 0.688 does not). Tested on fold 3 with the POC's cached HoVer-NeXt outputs: ECE 0.0223 with one view (T 1.42) and 0.0187 with 8 views (T 1.29), under the 0.05 bar. Rows live in CALIBRATIONS in model_catalogue.py until AIP-002 (model registry), and the least-sure-first order calibrates cells, not regions, at task creation. TODO: no endpoint sends the fields to REVIEW-014 yet, the displayed confidence is uncalibrated until AINUC-007, and refit when AINUC-002 produces outputs from the shipped weights.
- TILES-005 (range coalescing for sharded Zarr): the OME-Zarr reader wraps its store in zarrita's withRangeCoalescing. A 2048 square viewport of 64 inner chunks in 4 shards went from 72 requests to 12 through the latency proxy with the same bytes, the shape of doc 32's 194 to 39, and unsharded stores stay at 64. New synthetic sharded store passes the conformance suite. No time saving claimed, because Node's fetch has no six-connection limit. TODO: suffix ranges to drop the HEAD per shard (check R2 and the edge first), and measure the browser tile scheduler.
- JOBS-006 (show or hide Parfocal models): additive migration 0081 adds a per-workspace hidden_models table. GET /api/tenant/hidden-models is open to members, PUT hides and DELETE shows again with the workspace-settings permission, audited as model.hidden and model.shown. A hidden model leaves listProjectModels and cannot be started (model-hidden, also through a tier), and its result sets stay. The admin AI models switch calls hide or show with Undo, and an E2E hides CAMELYON and finds it gone from Run a model. GET /api/tenant/models is still draft-only.
- OBJ-005 (IDs on the slide and in lists): the engine places ID labels greedily over 16 candidate spots per anchor, rejecting any that hit another label, another anchor or the view edge, in a new id-labels overlay layer between measurements and comment pins. A new golden viv-overlay-id-labels.png and a regenerated viv-overlay-stack.png, with browser tests that labels never overlap or cover an anchor and hide with their layer. The Display tab ID labels switch now works, and ObjectIdTag copies the full path (P07/~/slide/T2, ~ for a loose slide, which the Full ID row used to leave empty). The ui Playwright config now honours PARFOCAL_E2E_PORT. Some web specs flake under heavy parallel load and pass alone.
- VIEW-015 (Leica SCN multi-pyramid): without a slide index the browser reader reads the SCN header in the first page's description and opens the one image whose view is smaller than the whole slide, refusing files with several such images with MultiRegionScnError as ingest does. Non-SCN files still open their largest image. Tested on the committed synthetic-decoy.scn (175 KB, a 1536x2048 macro larger than the 1024x768 slide) and the cached OpenSlide Leica-1.scn and Leica-3.scn. Cost 0.
- SKEL-007 (draw and save one polygon): P or a new rail button arms a polygon tool on the live viewer, closing on the first vertex, a double-click or Enter. Each closed polygon is posted to the existing annotation API as a region and loaded back when the slide opens, with no API change or migration. The real-stack E2E in apps/e2e/tests/polygon.spec.ts draws, reloads and checks the stored PostGIS points, passing on the Mac. It needs ports 5173 and 8000 for the Zitadel redirect and fails if an api:dev without TILE_TOKEN_KEY is already running. A fake-API spec in apps/web covers it too. TODO: moves, reshapes and deletes are not sent to the API yet, and a failed annotation list shows nothing.
- Ticked after CI run 41736409298 on cb482583, where the browser (web) job passed with e2e/view-transitions.spec.ts, magnification-bar.spec.ts and viewer-tools.spec.ts: SHELL-007 (view transitions), NAV-003 (slide brightness dial), NAV-005 (edge cues), NAV-010 (whole-slide peek) and NAV-004 (area list and go to area, whose prototype E2E passed in the same job). The run as a whole was cancelled by a newer push.
- GUIDE-005 (decisions in the full viewer): K, X and U and the Objects tab rows already worked. The gap was one review across modes: decisions lived only in each screen's state and the fake API forgot them. The fake now remembers saved and cleared decisions, a shared use-decision-saves hook saves and refreshes in guided review, the full viewer and focus view, Undo and Agree with the rest reach the server, and the Guided and Full viewer toggle keeps the selected object. Three E2E tests check the same decisions and counts in both modes. Notes and reasons stay on screen only until GUIDE-010.
- ONB-002 (sign-up and email confirmation): /sign-up creates the account, shows a 6-digit code step with resend and change address, and lands on the setup role step, as single sign-on does. The form reports missing name, bad email, short, reused or leaked password, missing consent and an existing account. Three draft calls with fake handlers, and the password, code and countdown inputs now shared with sign-in in auth-inputs.tsx. The sign-up browser spec replaces the old placeholder test. Mock diffs 21.2 percent (signup/form) and 18.8 percent (signup/verify), forms start empty.
- SKEL-012 (viewer takes the slide format from ingest): the tile-access read, the one the live viewer makes, now returns the format ingest stored in slides.format (null when none), and the slide stage passes it to signedOpenRequest, falling back to the file extension only when the format is missing or unknown. An API test sees generic-tiff for a slide stored as original.svs, and a viewer test opens a generic TIFF stored as slide.ndpi that the extension alone fails to open. No migration.
- ADJ-007 (queue decisions in the task's style): the researcher answers a queued object with the task's own decision buttons, and the API stores the raw answer and the value it counts as, with old keep-the-AI and go-with-the-reviewers rows reading back as agree and disagree. A queued object can be left open with an optional note (task states carry a left_open count, sign-off allows it, the evaluation treats it as undecided). Not sure with Need a second look queues the object tagged second-look. An object redrawn after submission is tagged edited, and the researcher keeps the decisions or asks both reviewers to look again, which marks their decisions reopened and blocks sign-off until they answer. A queue decision now overrides even a unanimous reviewer value. Additive migration 0080, ADR auto-numbered. The new code extends the existing verdicts table and module until FOUND-024 renames them. TODO: review screens must show the reopened decision and its note.
- ONB-001 (sign-in screens): password sign-in, single sign-on first when the email domain matches an institution (otherwise an institution picker), a sign-in link with a code, and forgot password with a reset checked for length, reuse and leaks, all against draft auth calls in the fake API that stand in for the Zitadel-hosted flows of IAM-002. A same-site return_to deep link survives every path and outside targets are ignored. Nine new E2E tests. The secrets allowlist covers only the made-up fake passwords and codes in three files. Inputs start empty, the SSO button uses a new institution short_name, the resend label counts down.
- ANN-004 (select tool): a pure state machine in apps/web/src/annotations/select-tool.ts with hit testing and reshape helpers, a SelectLayer in the slide stage, armed by V or the rail button. Click selects, Shift+click toggles under 3 px of movement, a marquee selects shapes it touches, a drag moves with Shift locking an axis, vertex and edge drags reshape with Shift snapping to 45 degrees, double-click adds or removes a point down to three, and Escape restores. 38 tests replay each gesture in the trace format. Doc 16 is not in the repo, so the agent's choices are in docs/knowledge/frontend-ui.md. The ruler is the only shape the app draws today. A commit(before, after, label) hook waits for ANN-013 (undo and redo).
- VIEW-049 (tissue box delivered to the viewer): the real opener createPixelSlideOpener takes a tissueUrl, fetches the tissue.json beside the slide while the pixel source opens and passes its box as the engine slide's tissue. A box passed by the caller still wins. The box is not in the tile index because ingest writes the index before it derives the tissue mask. A missing, broken, null or failed box opens fitted to the whole scan. Tested through the real opener with a synthetic TIFF. TODO: apps/web does not use the real opener yet, so CASE-016 wires tissueUrl.

## 2026-11-03

- JOBS-003 (jobs tray): the tray from PROTO-005 already existed. Failed rows now read Failed in red and cancelled rows Cancelled in warn, where both showed Done in green, and import jobs get a link icon. Five Storybook component specs cover the ring and every row state, seven E2E tests open an upload, a paused run and a finished run from the tray. Only the newest upload opens its steps, older ones open their project. TODO: JobRow's progress bar takes the detail text as its name instead of the title, for JOBS-004 or an accessibility pass.
- CASE-014 (inspector tabs): a kit InspectorPanel gives the full viewer Objects, Display, Comments and Opinion tabs, each with a count (objects, result sets, open threads, unanswered questions), and a template line that names the analysis template or says none. Staging cards still follow the template's viewer parts. The tab stays Display, not Layers, as the frozen mock and ADR 0132 name it. SidePanel gained a dense option so four counted tabs fit 340 px. Seeded component spec, unit tests and the full viewer spec pass. Mock diff for case/objects unchanged at 48.7 percent.
- ADMIN-001 (admin page shell): the sidebar and sections already existed. Added the permission gate: reviewers and researchers without admin rights see a short refusal with Back to Parfocal and no admin data is requested, and the main sidebar hides the Admin link from them. Seven unit tests and six Playwright specs. Mock diff for admin/overview 9.8 percent, layout lines up.
- Review fixes for the PR stack 4, 5 and 6: six parallel reviewers found 13 issues at the tip, a planner re-confirmed them and two implementers fixed 12 as one commit each on claude/next-20-tasks. AI areas rank by the shown size (CASE-004), P and Shift+A reach the review rail and = records as + (CMD-007), Enter on a focused My work row no longer opens the hero (WL-005), setup refreshes the home layout and home waits for it (HOME-010), comment drafts with no thread survive pan and zoom, the presence row holds while saving and the fake availability rejects what the API rejects (SHELL-012), a disabled radio group dims its rows (DS-007), Enter runs the flow primary action (DS-023), and the layout shift, brightness clamp, account menu order and motion tests were tightened. Deferred: Shift+F zoom to selection waits on NAV-013 and class keys 1 to 9 on ANN-003, both still listed in the keymap. TODO: the DS-029 focus ring check passes vacuously for 68 stories (disabled controls and open overlays lose the first Tab), which needs its own harness task.
- NAV-010 CI fix: the tap test held Z while it checked two locators, which on a slow runner took longer than the 350 ms tap window, so the release counted as a hold and closed the peek. It now checks the release hint during a full hold, then taps with a quick press.
- SHELL-007 CI fix, second pass: the router resolves its update before React draws the slide page, so the transition looked for [data-vt-target] too early, found nothing, and the opening move had no target. It now waits up to 300 ms for the target to render (targetWaitMs), with unit tests. In Chromium 2026 both elementFromPoint and real pointer events land on the html element for the whole transition, even with pointer-events none on ::view-transition. So the e2e now checks that a pointer move during the move reaches the page's document listeners, which is where the cut listens. Clicks on page controls during the move still land on html, not the control.
- SHELL-007 CI fix: the router changes the URL first and starts the view transition only after the slide route's loaders finish, so the open test read data-vt right after the URL changed and found nothing. The test now waits frame by frame for data-vt before checking the move and the cut. Not verified here, because the guided route's loaders never finish in this container.
- DS-005 CI fix: following the system theme ran the web axe suite in light for the first time, and the light warn tag on the page surface measured 4.3:1. The light --warn token moved from the mock's #8a5300 to #804d00, which gives 4.73:1 on the tag and 5.97:1 on the page. Also fixed for CI: the DS-023 stepper kept a locked step's reason inside the button name, so every Next locator matched several buttons, and the WL-006 row click swallowed Nudge and Reassign.
- CASE-006 (AI state: no tumor flagged): with no areas the staging card shows a Needs review chip (never negative), a Tissue scanned coverage bar and Start systematic scan, and each Next field click or ] press moves the camera one 10x field along the tissue in reading order. Logic in packages/ui lib/systematic-scan.ts (path, coverage, step), the card in components/systematic-scan.tsx, and a stand-in tissue mask traced from the mock overview.jpg on a 50 pixel grid in apps/web review/tissue-mask.ts. NAV-004's area keys still work on slides with areas. systematic-scan.test.ts (5), the analysis-cards spec (6 of 6) and apps/web/e2e/no-tumor-scan.spec.ts (on retry after the first Vite build) pass. The merge kept both imports in analysis-cards.tsx, and web and ui typecheck and the full ui (154) and web (487) unit suites pass after it. The full-viewer G then n zoom check fails here with and without the change. Added CASE-016 for the real tissue mask.
- NAV-010 (whole-slide peek): the progress ring's Whole slide button opens the peek and closes its popover, a click on the peek goes to that place and is saved in view history so the back key returns, and the peek ticks the areas actually decided instead of always T1. The Z key logic moved into a pure step in peek-model.ts with 7 unit tests (hold, a tap under 350 ms latches, Z or Esc closes, a long press on a latched peek stays closed). Hold, tap, minimap click and click to go existed from the PROTO port. The unit, area-nav and full-viewer tests (25) and web and ui typecheck pass, and of 3 new viewer-tools.spec.ts tests the ring and click to go then back pass, while tap to latch and the existing hold test fail here waiting 5 s for the peek or minimap button, like the unrelated focus-mode tests. In prototype navigator scenes a click records a camera but leaving remounts the viewer and wipes history.
- CASE-013 (AI state: only a small cluster): aiState() has a small-cluster kind when the lead area is at or under 0.2 mm (ADR 0086), and areas can carry a tumor cell count range. The case settles only once the lead is confirmed, measured and its count range cannot cross 200, and a count wholly over 200 makes it micro. The hero follows the POC Only a small cluster scene (proto.js stageHero, since mock 13 dropped the small scene) and lists the open steps (confirm, measure, count) in its text and as a step list. Three new stories (open, measured, settled), ai-state.test.ts and analysis-cards.spec.ts pass with the typography spec, axe on every analysis-cards story and ui typecheck. formatMm now shows 2 decimals below 1 mm everywhere (0.18 mm, not 0.2 mm) as the POC did, and the full apps/web (480) and packages/ui (149) unit suites pass after the merge. No cell counts reach the web viewer until AINUC-012, so the count step stays open there.
- DS-023 (flow page with the shared stepper): the stepper takes a furthest-step reached value so a visited step reopens from either side, an unreachable step keeps aria-disabled with its reason in the accessible description and tooltip, Arrow, Home and End move through steps, and the new useFlowSteps hook never jumps past reached plus one. The bar's shadow turns on when more content follows, FlowBar's new primary prop makes a disabled Next name its reason or show a Before you list for several blockers, and an overflowing body becomes a focusable Step content region for axe. Stories cover first, middle, revisited middle, blocked Next, long and last steps, with working Change links. packages/ui/e2e/flow-page.spec.ts (8 plus axe on 6 stories) and the flow page axe stories in both themes (38) pass, with ui typecheck and tests and web typecheck. The main column keeps the mock's 20 px gap (decisions.md 61). Added DS-037 for app flow screens to keep their step and draft.
- DS-004 (icon set with key badges): every icon has a spoken name (iconLabels, and label alone gives the default), a Foundations/Icons story group shows every icon, the five sizes, colour use and tool keys, and packages/ui/test/icon-scale.test.ts fails on an icon off the 12, 16, 20, 24, 40 scale or mixed stroke widths. Tool rail badges and tooltips take their key from the registry through an action prop, and the live slide ruler uses truler instead of a written-in L. Breadcrumb and checkbox svgs went from 3.5 to 12 px, and the avatar badge svg is hidden at default size and 12 px at large. No new dependency. ui and web typecheck and tests and e2e/icons.spec.ts (3) pass. tool-rail-button.spec.ts and focus-ring.spec.ts did not run while another agent's Playwright run held the machine.
- DS-003 (typography): the IBM Plex woff2 files are committed in packages/tokens/fonts (228 KB with OFL.txt) with font-display optional (ADR 0227), title sizes default to weight 600, Tailwind's default sizes are cleared, and packages/ui/test/type-scale.test.ts fails on any font size off the scale in the kit, its stories and apps/web, exempting printed report pages and glyphs inside avatars, rings and pins with reasons. About 150 off-scale sizes moved onto scale tokens. packages/ui/e2e/typography.spec.ts checks the type specimen story's size, line height, weight and real Plex rendering in both themes, and that a slow font never moves text. Tokens, ui and web typecheck and unit tests pass, and the type-scale scan passes on the merged branch. 1360 of 1984 kit browser tests passed before the time limit and a rerun of the spacing and size specs passed 468, so focus-ring and later specs did not run. Added DS-036 to preload the two main fonts.
- CASE-005 (AI state: still running): the staging card has a running state on aiState(). Found areas stay in the order found with a placeholder row in a fixed-height list, so no row moves, the map's dashed line follows the regions step percent, and the fake job moves that percent on each poll while streaming. A new test in apps/web/e2e/layout-shift.spec.ts covers areas appearing, the line moving, a steady viewBox and zero layout shift, and passes with the other layout-shift tests (one on retry), ui tests, the touched api-client test and ui, web and api-client typecheck. NAV-004 area navigation gets only the found areas while running, and the sets the category note is hidden then. Found regions are not drawn on the map, because the fake shapes sit below the reach line. Five full-viewer.spec.ts tests failed on slow page loads (three passed at a 30 s expect timeout), and the case/running shot differs on 47.6% with no baseline. The api-client scenario ID test that WL-005 broke by moving the resume point to T5 was fixed separately.
- DS-005 (theme switching): the theme lives on the settings device layer (localStorage key parfocal.device-settings in apps/web/src/shell/device-settings.ts) instead of parfocal-theme, defaults to system as the catalogue does, follows the OS preference live and applies before the first render. An old parfocal-theme value is copied once and deleted, and the root gets data-theme like data-density. Chrome tokens are tested for near-zero chroma in both themes. theme.test.ts, the tokens test and the storage-key policy test pass with web and tokens typecheck, and the new apps/web/e2e/theme.spec.ts passes with a 90 s timeout for the cold Vite compile. cookies-and-storage.md lists both keys, e2e/shot.ts writes the new key, and onboarding opens on the device's theme instead of always Dark. Developer mode and the pinned slide strip keep their own keys for SET-002.
- SHELL-007 (view transitions): a runner in apps/web/src/shell/view-transitions.ts replaces the router's startViewTransition. My work rows grow into the slide stage, a cell tile grows into the focused cell, and returning to My work crossfades. Any key or pointer press cuts a running transition, the transition layer has pointer-events none, and reduced motion (the system setting or data-motion=reduced) or no browser support changes instantly. Durations come from the motion tokens (400 ms stage, 300 ms default group, 200 ms crossfade), so presenting slows them by three, and the same cut was added for data-motion=reduced beside DS-016's media query. The merge conflicted with WL-019 on all-slides-tab.tsx, kept the virtual table and added data-vt-source to its rows. view-transitions.test.ts passes 14 of 14 after the merge with web typecheck. The new e2e/view-transitions.spec.ts never ran, because Playwright hung on fake API requests, as home-and-work.spec.ts did with the change removed. Submit back to My work only crossfades, because nothing names the row the slide came from.
- WL-019 (All slides tab): the All slides table is virtualised with the existing @tanstack/react-virtual dependency, keeping the native table with spacer rows above and below the view, measuring each row's height and using the shell's scroll area so the mock layout stays. A double click opens the slide, and J, K and Enter still work through virtual rows. ?bulk=N in the fake API adds N rows named bulk_00001 onward. New apps/web/e2e/all-slides.spec.ts passes 5 of 5 (10,000 rows with under 80 in the DOM, J moving 60 rows with the selection in view, K to the last row, search narrowing, double click opening a slide), all-slides-filters passes (one test on retry), and web and api-client typecheck pass. Mock shots: worklist/all 4.1% (4.0% before) and allpreview 7.2% (12.0% before). case-loader and home-and-work lines 5 and 32 (the greeting and date line) failed on retry, outside this table and not checked against the base.
- REVIEW-012 (outline display modes): fade, mark unsure and bands are overlay data in viewer-engine overlay/outline-modes.ts, using ADR 0194's fade formula (0.12 + 0.88 x strength) and band cutoffs (80, 55, 40). Unsure calls (top under 40% or within 10 points of the second) draw as white dashes on a near-black casing one overlay order above. Band alphas are 1, 0.55 and 0.22 from the mock plus 0.12, the fade floor, for the fourth band. LineCasing.followsAlpha in overlay/deck/overlay-layers.ts lets the casing take each outline's alpha, and only the Viv route draws overlays. New golden spec viv-outline-modes.spec.ts on the synthetic slide (goldens viv-outline-fade, viv-outline-mark-unsure, viv-outline-bands, plus difference, strength and white checks) passes with the brightness spec (13), and outline-modes and outline-casings node tests and viewer-engine and golden-images typecheck pass. Notes in golden-images README.md and knowledge/viewer-build.md. Added REVIEW-027 to connect it in the review viewer.
- REVIEW-022 (AI wording on screen) adds frequency to the personal setting confidence_wording with no migration and an aiWording helper in packages/ui confidence-words.ts mapping each setting value to a wording mode. The GalleryTile ai prop, a new inspector AiCallLine, the guided review card and the cells grid tiles (apps/web review/ai-wording.ts) read their text through it, and a typer with no calibration evidence falls back to percent as ADR 0194 says. ai-wording.spec.ts, gallery-tile.spec.ts, the confidence-words and ai-wording unit tests, the api-client settings resolution test and apps/api test_settings.py pass, with ui, web and api-client typecheck. Because the review fake has no calibration evidence, the Word preset (preset 3 on RT-14) now shows the percent where it said very sure, so REVIEW-024 now asks for fake calibration evidence. Added REVIEW-026 for the Settings row and guided review following the setting.
- DS-033 (density for page gaps and list rows) adds --density-page-gap (22 to 14 px, from .wl-main) for Home's page gaps, --density-flow-gap (16 to 14 px, from .wl-main.pf-main) for the Evaluation, Team, Queue and My work pages, and --density-item-y (10 to 6 px, the compact value an agent choice as the mock has none) for the My work comment and ask rows and the disagreement evidence rows. Project slide and task rows and SettingRow use --density-list-row-y, and the My work Assigned and All slides cells use --density-cell-y. The density spec checks the page gap, setting row padding and a shorter compact list in both modes (3 pass), and the tokens test (17) and tokens, ui and web typecheck and tests pass. The project and My work table headers, the project slide set rows and the Team reviewer rows stay fixed, now part of DS-034.
- SHELL-009 (screen coverage measurement): apps/web/e2e/chrome-share.ts measures the share of the viewport covered by the top bar, tool rail and side panel, counting overlaps once. chrome-share.spec.ts measures Review (the full viewer with a task) at 1280, 1440 and 1920 by 900 and fails above 35% at 1440x900, the only width the KPI names, and runs in CI through pnpm test:browser. viewer-tools.spec.ts uses the utility for focus mode (above 0 and under 35% before, exactly 0 in focus), replacing SHELL-008's in-place chromeShare. chrome-share.test.ts (6) passes with web typecheck. The spec passed at all three widths with one worker (1280 on retry) and the focus tests with raised timeouts at load near 24. No measured number went into a result file.
- CASE-004 (AI state: pre-read ready): the staging panel has an open and a settled state on a shared aiState() helper in packages/ui lib/ai-state.ts, which returns running, no-tumor or pre-read with ranked areas, the lead, the category and the suggested pN for CASE-005, CASE-006 and CASE-013. Open leads with Metastasis likely, largest about 7.7 mm, the Macro if confirmed chip and the size scale with the 0.2 and 2 mm lines, areas numbered largest first, and Start with area 1 goes through NAV-004's area navigation. Settled reads Macrometastasis, 8.0 mm, settled with pN1a(sn), smaller rows say Cannot change the category, and Submit opens the Opinion tab. Wording comes from a new findingWords() on confidence-words.ts with a percentage fallback for an uncalibrated model, and a confident score reads likely, never found, before confirmation. Warnings show through ModelWarningsBlock. The merge with NAV-004 was resolved on the task branch keeping both, with the row tag back on the object ID. ai-state.test.ts (5), analysis-cards.spec.ts (6) and area-nav.test.ts (11) pass with ui and web typecheck. The canvas has no numbered area badges yet.
- REVIEW-021 (comment tool pins a thread on click): in comment mode (C) a click on the slide drops a pin and opens New thread at the pin in the Comments tab, posting saves through the existing useCreateThread API with a point anchor and refreshes the list, Esc or Cancel leaves comment mode without a thread, and a drag past 4 px still pans. Logic in review/comment-pin.ts, composer and pin marker in pinned-thread.tsx, SlideStill gained onPointPick and full-viewer.tsx is wired. comment-pin.test.ts (9) and 2 new review-rail.spec.ts tests (pin under the click, Esc) pass, with web typecheck. Added REVIEW-025 because the fake has no POST threads override.
- WL-005 (To review tab): rows come from a tested row model in to-review-rows.ts, each showing project, scope, due date, the Blind tag, my progress and a status tag with Continue or Start. Continue and the Continue card open guided review at the next open object through a new object URL param that GuidedReview now honours, and Enter with no row selected continues the task in progress. The fake resume point now names T5, the first open object. to-review-rows.test.ts covers in progress, new and finished tasks and the Enter action, and a new E2E in home-and-work.spec.ts goes from My work to guided review at T5 and checks object=T3 focuses T3. Web typecheck, the api-client fake and work tests and the worklist-keys and My work E2Es pass. The shot differs from the mock on 4.9% of pixels because each row's second line adds scope as the task asks. The long every home and My work scene E2E ran out of time at load 28.
- CMD-007 (every shortcut fires on the person's own keys): a PersonalKeys context in apps/web/src/keys/personal-keys.ts gives every hotkey hook the person's preset and own keys, covering answer, note, rail, viewer, class, peek, compare and palette keys, and the answer keys go through one hook in review/answer-keys.ts for guided review, the full viewer and focus mode. Overlays keeps A (QuPath and the frozen mock), AI tools moves to ⇧A, Annotate is the new action annotate on P in the review scope only, and Hide the side panel uses the existing panel action on I, dropping the stale ] hint. own-keys.test.ts rebinds an answer key, the certainty digits, the note key and Annotate and checks each fires on the new key only, and another test finds no shared default key in any scope or decision style. Web typecheck and tests pass. Guided review and disagreements specs timed out on page loads at load near 30, and guided review passed alone with long timeouts. SHELL-005 now notes that I cannot hide the panel until ViewerFrame has a hidden state.
- DS-032 (steady axe run on animated stories): the endless shortcut key pulse never stopped, so the wait for animations gave up after 2 s and axe read contrast mid fade, failing key-states 5 of 20 runs. One line, test.use({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' }) in packages/ui/e2e/axe.spec.ts, turns off the motion-safe pulse while the story is still checked. key-states then passed 10 runs in a row in both themes (20 of 20), the full axe spec passed 683 of 683 with 2 workers, and ui typecheck is clean.
- REVIEW-023 (model warnings in the inspector) adds ModelWarningsBlock in packages/ui components/model-warnings.tsx, a Zone titled Model warnings listing each modelWarnings result as a WarnBar note in rule order with a count when there are several, and showing nothing with no warnings or with the Model warnings setting off. model-warnings.stories.tsx and e2e/model-warnings.spec.ts cover none, one, several and the toggle (4 pass), with ui typecheck. No screen has the typer metadata or the model_warnings setting yet, so REVIEW-024 was added. Empty state question in decisions.md 60.
- NAV-004 CI fix: the go to area test needs about 25 wheel steps to reach z 16, and toPass sent one per second, so it ran out of its 30 s. It now sends eight spaced steps per attempt, waits for the 250 ms camera write, and allows 90 s.
- DS-028 (decision buttons in the four decision styles) released without work. It was claimed as the thirtieth task of the next-30-tasks batch, but DS-018 took HOME-003's place in that count, so it never started.
- NAV-004 (area list and go to area) in the full viewer: the area list runs largest first with category and role, G then n, [ (prevarea) and ] (nextarea), clickable rows (RegionRow, StagingCards in packages/ui), a visited mark at 10x or more, jumps recorded in view history and the URL object set as ADR 0176 says. The lead is the largest area not marked disagree, and settled means the lead is agreed. New area-nav.ts and use-area-nav.ts, stepArea reuses neighbourTargets from edge-cues.ts so the cues share the order, and createViewHistory<View>(limit, same) in viewer-engine is now generic. area-nav.test.ts (11), the generic history test and web, ui and viewer-engine typecheck pass. A new full-viewer.spec.ts E2E passes on the still prototype canvas only with raised timeouts at load 35, and the two prototype index sweeps hit 120 s. On the live route the keys do not reach the engine, so NAV-014 was added.
- DS-026 (table) adds sortable column headers that set aria-sort and announce their order, a checkbox column with a header box for none, some or all, a selected prop on rows, number and ID cell kinds (right-aligned tabular figures, monospace) and one empty row that says why. Sorting and picking logic lives in packages/ui lib/table-state.ts, and the DS-017 density padding is unchanged. e2e/table.spec.ts passes 8 of 8 (sorting, tabbing every row's checkbox and Open, Space and Enter, the mixed header box, picked and hover backgrounds in both themes, the empty row), axe and the focus ring pass on the new stories in both themes, and unit tests and ui typecheck pass. Added DS-034 to move app tables onto it (the object table rows are mouse only) and DS-035 for the amber warn tag at 4.3:1 in light.
- DS-025 (tabs): tab stories cover page, side panel and quiet tabs with and without counts, and the new tabs.spec.ts checks arrow keys with wrap-around, Home and End, Enter, a disabled tab that takes focus but never opens (ARIA allows it), the panel focus ring and count styles in both themes (7 of 7). TabCount matches the mock, a 16 px badge in panels and plain faint numbers in page tabs, and My work and the project page use it instead of their own markup. Axe and the focus-ring spec pass 104 of 104 on the tab, team, My work and project stories in both themes, and ui and web typecheck and tests pass.
- DS-024 (form fields with labels, hints and inline errors): in packages/ui form-field.tsx the help line or error sits in one shared footer under the field. Added TextAreaInput with characterCount, CheckboxField and RadioField (a legend over the DS-007 RadioRow), and an invalid field draws a red ring on focus on top of DS-029. Stories in form-fields.stories.tsx and form-fields.spec.ts check every label, help line and error through the accessibility tree and that an error never clears typed text (8 of 8, new-forms 5 of 5), and axe and the focus-ring spec pass on the form stories in both themes (92). ui and web typecheck pass. A radio row is now named by its title, with its subtitle as description, and one new-forms test changed for it. Required labels read as part of the name, such as Stain Required.
- DS-018 (surfaces and zones): the zone spacing tokens --space-zone, --space-in, --pad-zone and --pad-modal sit in the density blocks beside --density-*, with comfortable at 28, 10, 18 and 24 and compact at 24, 8, 14 and 24. surfaceLevels, surfaceLevelTokens, surfaceSpacingPx and surfaceSpacingRulesPx hold the rules (8 to 12 px inside a zone, 24 to 32 px between zones, at least 24 px modal padding), checked by tokens tests. New Components/Surfaces stories show every level in both themes and a modal with plain, highlighted and folded zones, reusing the existing Surface, Zone and FoldingZone. e2e/surfaces.spec.ts measures paddings, gaps, distinct backgrounds and the fold summary in both themes, and axe passes. Compact now visibly tightens zones in every panel, to check at the next screen review.
- DS-015 (skeletons and placeholders): the skeleton pulse runs on the motion tokens and stops under reduced motion, and Tailwind's animate-pulse uses them. The case inspector holds a skeleton row where the next region lands, polls AI progress every 3 s while running and waits for progress before showing, and AI steps keep an empty slot for their progress bar so rows do not move. The fake ?stream=1 adds one region per read. New apps/web/e2e/layout-shift.spec.ts reports zero layout shift while findings stream and checks the 2 s pulse and its stop under reduced motion, passing on a warm server and timing out once cold at load 30. The fixed-duration test now also catches animate-[...] durations, which moved the shortcut editor's 1.2 s pulse onto animate-pulse. api-client, tokens and motion-durations tests and web, ui and api-client typecheck pass. full-viewer.spec.ts 'every full viewer and Visualize scene opens' timed out here waiting for the prototype index, likely load, to rerun in CI. The full-screen Loading the slide text is still plain text.
- ANN-011 (classes and keys): one pickClass in apps/web/src/annotations/classes.ts serves the swatch click and the new registry action aclass (keys 1 to 9 in the annotate scope, Tools group in keymap.ts). With nothing selected it sets the class for new shapes, with a selection it re-classes them except ruler, arrow, text and locked groups, and in review it marks model shapes agreed or disagreed and returns teach examples. annotation-classes.test.ts checks key and swatch give the same result, no clashes in Annotate, and that review never binds aclass (digit 3 still fires dcert:3). Web typecheck and tests pass, and a frontend-ui.md knowledge entry was added. The useClassKeys hook has no caller until ANN-003 wires the swatch strip, now noted there. Key range in decisions.md 50.
- SHELL-012 (account menu) reads and sets availability through getMyAvailability and setMyAvailability. Initials only sits in a new shell store (apps/web/src/shell/shell-store.ts, useSyncExternalStore because zustand could not be added) and changes the menu name at once, theme applies at once, Log out goes to /api/auth/logout, and unsent comment drafts are kept per user in sessionStorage (parfocal.drafts, added to the cookies and storage policy) across logging out and back in. Avatars use personFromProfile. A component spec per item with a keyboard walk, axe on both account stories in both themes, a web E2E logging out and back in with the draft intact, unit and fake availability tests and web, ui and api-client typecheck pass. Help and what's new (SHELL-013 added) and Lock the screen (decisions.md 49) are disabled. SHELL-002 now replaces the store with Zustand, WL-013 reads initialsOnly, and theme stays on the old parfocal-theme key for SET-002.
- REVIEW-014 (model warnings) adds modelWarnings and slideStatistics in packages/ui lib/model-warnings.ts following ADR 0194: a typer under 70% on its test set, an uncalibrated typer, a confident share below 0.6 of the test set's (when that is at least 20%), an unsure share over 15 points above the test set's, and a site outside the typer's validated list. The share rules need at least 100 typed cells, missing metadata raises nothing, and all thresholds come from typer metadata. model-warnings.test.ts passes 11 of 11 on the CMU-1 example (23% confident against 59%) with ui typecheck. Added REVIEW-023 to show them in the inspector, and AINUC-006 now names the typer metadata to store.
- HOME-003 (resume state) released without code. Storing the per-user resume state (task, slide, camera, mode, object and when it was written, preferred over derived activity when newer per ADR 0085) needs a new resume_states table, one row per tenant and user, behind PUT /api/me/resume and read by /api/me/home, with migration 0080 after 0079_task_invitations. personal_settings does not fit, because set_my_settings checks every key against the catalogue (ADR 0138), shares one updated_at and would expose resume data through GET /api/me/settings. This batch takes no migrations, so the next agent builds it with migration 0080.
- DS-017 (density modes) follows the ADR 0187 data attribute pattern. tokens.css has --density-* variables for comfortable on :root and [data-density=comfortable] and for compact on [data-density=compact] from the frozen mock's .app.compact rules, mirrored in densitySpacingPx and densityMode in @parfocal/tokens. Table rows and headers, the SidePanel inspector body and foot and the My work to-review rows use them, and comfortable gives the same pixels as before. apps/web shell/density.ts (applyDensity, useAppliedDensity) is mounted in __root.tsx and writes data-density from the existing personal density setting, with no migration, and the Settings switch applies at once. packages/ui/e2e/density.spec.ts, the tokens test and apps/web/test/density.test.ts pass with ui, tokens and web typecheck and tests. Added DS-033 for page gaps and the other list rows.
- CASE-015 (progress ring in the slide top bar): the ring split by agree, disagree, not sure and open, its popover (task, slide n of N, due date, time left, Previous and Next) and the top bar wiring existed from DS-019 and the PROTO port, so this added tests. New stories for an empty, a partial and a finished slide in review-popovers.stories.tsx and packages/ui/e2e/top-bar-ring.spec.ts check the percentage, one arc per colour, the check at 100%, the popover, Next opening slide 9 and Next off on the last slide (3 new and 4 progress-meter specs pass). The app E2E 'the full viewer ring shows Next slide' passes on the prototype full viewer route, and the guided ring test in the same file failed once at 30 s and passed on retry.
- NAV-005 (edge cues): cues are placed by the pure function edgeCues in viewer-tools/edge-cues.ts instead of fixed spots. Each sits on the edge facing its area, shows the distance in mm from the view centre (reproducing the mock's 12.0 and 13.2 mm, so the hard-coded distanceMm table is gone), moves aside for others on the same edge, hides when its area is on screen, and flies to the area on click. Cues point at the areas before and after the current one, fade in with the motion tokens and show a focus ring, and minimap placement avoids them. 8 unit tests in edge-cues.test.ts, viewer-tools.test.ts, motion-durations and web and ui typecheck pass. A new E2E clicks the T1 cue and expects object=T1, but viewer-tools.spec.ts fails before any cue in this container (the Screens list has 0 links), as does the unchanged Area 3 cue test.
- LAB-004 (pan sweep benchmark and export) adds apps/web/src/lab/pan-sweep.ts, which pans 180 steps around an ellipse at the current zoom, waits 600 ms, restores the camera and records frame p50, p95 and fps. The other 13 of ADR 0189's 16 CSV columns come from getStats() when it exists and stay blank otherwise. The drawer's Benchmark section (bench-section.tsx) runs the sweep and exports a dated CSV, the Export button shows the real run count and is disabled at 0, and the debug handle gains runBenchmark, getSampleCount and csv. 8 unit tests in pan-sweep.test.ts pass with web typecheck. The sweep never ran on a real slide here, so no numbers and no result file exist. /developer is its own page, so Run only works once LAB-001 opens the drawer over the viewer, and the blank columns fill once LAB-002 provides getStats().
- REVIEW-011 (AI wording modes) adds packages/ui lib/confidence-words.ts with plain (default), percent and frequency modes over calibrated probabilities and the four ADR 0194 bands at 80, 55 and 40, with a close call when the top two are within 10 points. Plain phrases are tumor, probably tumor, possibly tumor, tumor or immune and unclear, perhaps A or B. Cutoffs and band accuracies come per typer, frequency quotes the measured band accuracy or says not measured at this confidence, and an uncalibrated or unchecked typer falls back to percent. 9 tests in confidence-words.test.ts pass with ui typecheck. Added REVIEW-022 to use it on screen and add frequency to the confidence_wording setting.
- DS-007 (segmented control, toggle, slider, stepper) restyles the switch and slider to the kit with the ADR 0187 motion tokens, brings back the focus ring on segments and the number stepper, and fixes a slider given a single number drawing two thumbs, which affected the brightness popover. Adds a shared RadioRow used by the new forms and a Controls/Choice controls story set with locked rows. choice-controls.spec.ts passes 6 of 6, axe passed 151 of 152 (the flaky key-states story, passing alone, see DS-032), ui typecheck and tests pass. - FOUND-025 (faster and less wasteful CI) ticked. The docs-only run 41642315361 on 7a9e221 compared with the last green head de5657c, found only docs, skipped db, formats, e2e and browser, and passed ci-ok in 1.2 min. A skipped matrix job shows its unexpanded name in the GitHub list, which is cosmetic.
- FOUND-025 (faster and less wasteful CI) first run, 41638999283 on de5657c: green in 22 min against 46 min for the last run before it. Browser legs ran in parallel: ui 16 min, web 12, viewer-engine 7, golden-images 3, latency-proxy under 1. db took 21 min against 30, with pytest at 17.6 min for 1749 tests. Its slowest tests are now spread out (listNotifications tenant isolation 27 s, random cells order 22 s, getProject project role 18 s), so no single cause is left.
- FOUND-025 (faster and less wasteful CI) built. ci.yml runs the browser suites as a matrix of one job per package (ui, viewer-engine, golden-images, web, latency-proxy) with fail-fast off, instead of turbo --concurrency=1 in one 46 min job where an early failure hid later suites. A new changes job compares the head with the last green push run on main (or the PR base) and skips db, formats, e2e and browser when only docs/ or Markdown changed, with docs/design counted as code. Comparing with the last green run, not the previous push, keeps a docs commit from greening code whose run was cancelled. ci now cancels a running ci on main when a newer push arrives, and ci-ok passes on skipped jobs but fails if the changes job did not succeed. The db job was slow because test_every_guarded_endpoint_follows_the_matrix_for_every_role rebuilt the whole app and its OpenAPI schema in every one of about 460 cases through guarded_operations(), and the cost grew as the run went on (0.8 s alone, up to 4.3 s at the end). Caching operations() and guarded_operations() with functools.cache cut test_permissions.py from about 7 min for the guarded cases alone to 1 min 44 s for all 703 tests on the M4, and the whole db suite to 8 min 48 s locally. pytest -m db in CI now prints the 25 slowest tests. Fable (model) proposed the options; the owner chose these four on 2026-11-03. FOUND-026 (web browser tests against a built app) holds the build mode idea.
- PROTO-007 (guided review) fix: the Slide done card started its opinion answers from session.opinion only at mount, so when the saved opinion arrived after mount its answers were ignored and a required question stayed 'not answered', which kept Submit disabled in 3 of 10 runs of the required scene. Edits now sit over the saved answers whenever they arrive. guided-review.spec.ts passed 180 of 180 repeats after the fix. No deterministic test, because the fake API answers inside the browser where page.route cannot delay it. PROTO-008 (scene smoke tests) follow-up: the eight page-loop smoke tests that ran near or past their timeouts on CI are split. Fixed lists (admin and onboarding, settings, guided review, upload scenes) became one test per scene, and lists read from an index page (issue states, full viewer, viewer tools, flow map boxes) run in four parts through apps/web/e2e/parts.ts. The web suite is 325 tests and ran in 4.6 min on the M4. TODO: find why every dev-mode page load got 1.5 to 1.9 times slower on CI since 2026-11-02 (about 340 module requests per load).
