- CI on main after the WebGPU fix: the WebGPU draw tests now pass on the Linux runner with zero delta (run 41599021552, Chrome 156), which let the apps/web browser suite run for the first time since about 22:50 on 2026-11-02. Three of its tests failed, also on the Mac. 'switching slides keeps the camera each slide had' from CASE-002 (slides popover with Q and E stepping) pressed Q 5 to 10 ms after the URL changed, before React re-rendered, so the handler still saw slide A1 and had no previous slide. It now waits for the A2 chip first, like the Q and E test above it, and passed 12 of 12 repeats. 'every box opens a route' (flow map) and 'every upload scene opens on fake data' loop over many pages and ran over the 30 s test timeout, because every web browser test got 10 to 75% slower since the last green run (flow map loop 20.8 s then). Both now get 90 s, as the slide camera test already did. TODO: find why the web pages got slower.
- STACK-012 (frontend testing stack) follow-up: the WebGPU SwiftShader draw tests failed on the Linux runner because Linux Chrome needs --use-vulkan=swiftshader --enable-features=Vulkan as well. Without them the adapter is found but onSubmittedWorkDone throws 'A valid external Instance reference no longer exists'. Reproduced in the Playwright 1.64.0 Linux arm64 container, where either flag alone still fails and both together pass all 3 tests with zero delta. The M4 still passes with them. Result and knowledge/tooling.md updated. TODO: tick INGEST-001 on the first green CI run on main.
- CI on main: the latency-proxy browser test expected proxy stats without the per-method counts INGEST-028 added, which kept main red from about 22:50 on 2026-11-02. Fixed in 9dc79ea. Behind it, the WebGPU SwiftShader draw tests from STACK-012 (frontend testing stack, dd81b8c) fail on the Linux runner with Chrome 156 ('A valid external Instance reference no longer exists'), also on a rerun, but pass on the Mac. The adapter test passes on both. TODO: make the two WebGPU draw tests work on the Linux runner, then tick INGEST-001 on the first green run. pnpm verify does not run test:browser, so merges do not catch browser suite failures.
- I18N-002 (locale-aware formatting): createFormat(settings) in packages/ui and useFormat() in apps/web format numbers, percentages, dates, times, durations, µm lengths and mm sizes from the language (en-GB or cs-CZ) and lengths settings, measurements without digit grouping and with tabular figures (ADR auto-numbered). Object sizes, the viewer facts card, result set dates and the project object count use it. Sizes under 1 mm now show two decimals (0.45 mm). TODO: move the rest onto it: viewer-frame sizes, the MeasurementFoot ruler, RunningObjects, report, evaluation, sign-off, disagreements, tasks-tab and object-comments dates, screens/format.ts (hard-coded en-GB), admin TB and percent, and the viewer-engine scale bar formatLength.
- PROJ-007 (new project and new case): NewProjectForm and NewCaseForm kit components wired to createProject and createCase. The project form takes a name, the privacy mode as radio rows with a sentence each, the analysis template and a class list (cell review switches to PanNuke unless picked by hand). Patient fields appear only in clinical projects. The fake API creates projects and cases with 422 and 409 refusals, and a New case item sits in the project menu. The dev Fake data badge no longer blocks clicks. TODO: Members and the report template zone wait for create fields, the Slides drop zone is CASES-002, and the mock's Birth number is sent as mrn until CASES-015.
- TASK-006 (invitations to a task): POST /api/review-tasks/{task_id}/invitations lets the project owner of an open task invite someone new as a reviewer, stored on invitations with a new nullable task_id (migration 0079, additive). The task-invitation email names who asked, the project, the task, the slide count and the due date in its body, the subject only the workspace, sent to the local sink. Accepting joins the project, assigns every task slide and lands on the first slide, or only joins the workspace if the task closed. The new-task wizard now sends its emailed invites after creating the task, which were silently dropped before. ADR auto-numbered. TODO: the invite landing still reads the draft preview endpoint (ONB-003), and a task of only invited people still fails because a task needs one reviewer.
- PROJ-005 (analysis templates): each project returns viewer_parts derived on the server from its template (ADR auto-numbered). Every template gets objects, layers, decisions and opinion, sentinel node adds staging hero, area list and measure step, cell review adds unsure zones and cell typing. The viewer shows a simple staging card above the tabs only for sentinel node projects. No migration and no new route. TODO: the measure step, unsure zones and cell typing are listed but not gated yet (MEAS-006 and the cell typing tasks), and REVIEW-004 and CASE-004 replace the simple card.
- NOTIF-002 (the bell): the panel's tabs, counts, Unread only, Mark all read and mark-on-open already existed. See all now closes the panel and goes to My work, since no notifications page is specified, and fake mention and reply links land on the Comments thread (/tasks/RT-14/comments?slide=A1&thread=T2). Panel component tests and a Home E2E that opens a mention and lands on the thread. Mock diff 6.0 percent, text rendering only.
- COLLAB-006 (mentions): an @[user-id] token in a thread message notifies that colleague, once per person and at most 20 per message, if they can open the task slide and see the thread (and, under blinding, the author may see them). Anyone else fails the message with 400 bad-mention. A mention breaks a mute and replaces the reply notice for followers. Picker route listMentionableMembers, no migration, ADR auto-numbered. The UI composer picker comes with the thread screens.
- CMD-002 (command palette): Cmd+K opens through the key registry's search action, matches loosely over actions, places (the search API, with path pieces such as s26-04812 c145 in the fake) and every settings row, shows keys from the keymap, runs Measure on a slide, and lists lab actions only in developer mode, which a Settings entry toggles in localStorage. Groups sort by their best match so Enter runs the top result. Five Playwright tests and nine unit tests. TODO: areas on the current slide are not listed yet, and registry actions without a screen handler are left out.
- VIEW-057 (BIF frame placement): ingest writes OpenSlide's confidence-weighted frame pitch into the sidecar and the browser reader places BIF frames on it with bilinear sampling, later frame on top. OS-1.bif error against OpenSlide fell from 52.35 and 65.40 grey levels to 0.57, and the seam ratio at the reader's tile grid from 2.88 and 3.09 to 0.92 and 0.99. At the real frame edges seams stay at 2.76 and 2.85, as OpenSlide's own. No BIF golden exists yet and ventana-bif stays unregistered in the browser reader, both for VIEW-014. Per-boundary placement asked in decisions.md.
- REVIEW-017 (tumor share result): doc 33's range against a lab cutoff, with the count of contested cells to check, drawn as an optional result chip on the kit NumberLine. Three lab settings: tumor_share_cutoff (20), tumor_share_result_sentinel_node (off) and tumor_share_result_pannuke (on). The switch follows the project's class list because a specimen type is free text (ADR 0163). Unit tests reproduce doc 33's examples and the hidden sentinel node case. No live review screen shows the chip until REVIEW-005 (number line) is built.
- INGEST-018 (ingest failure UX): each ingest failure code is transient or permanent (unknown codes are permanent), the upload status route carries failure_class, a refused file sends the uploader an upload-refused notification shown in the bell's Jobs tab (migration 0078, additive), and a kit IngestFailure component offers Try again or Contact support. Playwright component tests cover each class. The upload screens still run on prototype data, so it shows once INGEST-021 and INGEST-022 wire real status polling. support@parfocal.eu is a placeholder, asked in decisions.md.
- SEC-027 (edge limits per IP): the edge Worker limits login to 10 a minute, upload writes to 120 a minute and tiles to 1,000 per 10 s per CF-Connecting-IP with Cloudflare's free rate limiting binding, answering the same 429 rate-limited problem as the API (ADR 0088). Turnstile on login turns on with the TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY secret, kept unset until the web app shows the widget. Checked locally with wrangler dev: one address got 10 times 200 then 429, another got 200. The limits are guesses to tune from pilot traffic. TRUST_TEST_CLIENT_ADDRESS is for local wrangler dev only and must never be set when deployed. TODO: the preview test (PARFOCAL_RATE_LIMIT_URL=<url> node --test apps/edge/test/deployed-rate-limits.test.ts) after the first staging deploy, and the Worker does not forward /api to Modal yet.
- SET-011 (workspace settings console): an admin Workspace settings section for lock times (through the session policy), default privacy, default classes and default study template (a new validated id catalogue kind), model tiers and announcements. Every write is audited, and researchers without admin rights and reviewers get 403. No new routes or migration. At merge the console's fake announcement store was folded into the HOME-009 announcement board so Home and the console share one. TODO: project and task creation do not apply the defaults yet, and the tier picker lists the three Parfocal models from a static web list until a model catalogue endpoint exists.
- TILES-003 (same-origin data path): slide bytes stay on the app origin and the viewer refuses to send its token anywhere else. Chrome sent 0 preflights for 63 token-bearing reads, against a preflight on every read from another origin, which doubled the open. The open does not match INGEST-007 yet: 0.63 s in Chrome at 100 ms RTT against 0.35 to 0.39 s in Node, because the browser path has no block cache and rereads the coarsest directory. It is still under the 1 s budget. TileAccess now uses no-store, because Chrome's per-file cache lock queued parallel ranges (1.35 s). Moved to review: Done when asks for matching open times. TODO: a small-block read cache for the browser path, and the staging check after the first deploy.
- HOME-009 (announcements): admins post from the Home Announcements card with kind, text of 1 to 280 characters and a duration, and end them per row. The fake API keeps a shared store so Home and the list agree. Playwright covers non-admin and admin, unit tests cover end times and limits, api-client tests cover expiry. Mock diff for home/launch is 16.8 percent, not yet compared with the score before this change. Non-admins see the same markup as before.
- CASE-002 (slides popover): rows show block, processing and review status, Q and E step slides and stop at the ends, the full viewer keeps a camera per slide, and an optional pinned strip is kept in browser storage until settings can be saved. Side by side and Compare slides open the prototype compare scene until CMP-007, Add slides opens the upload page until INGEST-021. The annotate view does not keep cameras yet.
- INGEST-013 (derived overview levels): ingest writes the missing coarse levels as a lossless TIFF sidecar named in the tile index, and the viewer adds them to the slide's ladder. Coarse tile reads fell from 25 to 45 times a full ladder to 0.4 to 0.6 times on the three Aperio fixtures, sidecar 1.3 to 2.1 MB built in 0.3 to 0.4 s. The 2x and 8x steps between stored Aperio levels stay as they are. The browser only benefits once the web app opens slides through a served tile-index.json.
- VIEW-056 (guided input trace recorder) built: /spikes/input-traces walks a mouse, a Mac trackpad and an optional Windows touchpad through timed steps and downloads one schema 1 JSON per device for packages/viewer-engine/test/input/traces. Playwright drives every step, a unit test replays the JSON through the gesture interpreter. TODO: the owner records the real traces when a mouse is at hand, then VIEW-021 can be ticked. The viewer-engine README still points at the old traces:record page.
- GUIDE-018 (change Counts as on an existing task) adds a task settings drawer on the project's Tasks tab, where a Counts as change confirms with Recounts N of M answers and then shows in the report's Methods block. The fake PATCH recounts, refuses a signed-off task with 409 and a mapping with no agree or disagree answer with 422, and a seeded certainty task RT-13 carries the Playwright test.
- INGEST-009 (seams and correctness check) adds reference_tiles.py, which saves server-reader windows at two levels, and reader-check.ts, which compares the browser reader with them and runs the POC seam test, with a labelled failure for each re-introduced POC bug. Clean slides agree within 1 grey level (about 2 on CMU-1.tiff) with seam ratios of 1.1 to 1.7, ICC profiles stay unapplied in both readers, and the browser reader ignores BIF stitch positions (OS-1.bif off by 52 to 65 grey levels), which went to VIEW-057. Its reference tiles sit in folders named like slides, so .gitignore now lets that folder through.
- CMP-002 (linked views) adds a pure linked-views model to viewer-engine where panes move together and a relink keeps the manual offset in x, y, zoom and rotation, and wires it into the compare screen. Shift+L is the linkviews action in a new compare scope of the key registry, and the compare rotation control goes with VIZ-005.
- COLLAB-005 (comments tab) added a kit Comments tab with an Open and Resolved toggle with counts, thread cards, go to location and New comment on C, wired into guided review, the full viewer and comments on IDs and driven by useListThreads with new fakes. Object-level lookups still read the draft slide-threads hook until COLLAB-003 or COLLAB-004, and resolve, reopen and reply belong to the thread popover task.
- INGEST-008 (sparse tile and absent chunk map) adds runs of missing Zarr tiles per level to the version 1 sidecar, written by zarr_index.py, so the Zarr source never requests them and Viv paints them as background. TIFF absence stays as byte count 0, now tested on Philips-4. A missing chunk inside a shard still reads black and belongs to VIEW-017, and the Zarr indexer waits for OME-Zarr ingest.
- MEAS-001 (ruler tool) adds the ruler to the live slide, with length in µm or mm from the slide's microns per pixel, Shift snap to 45° and double-click snap to the maximum Feret diameter of the smallest closed shape under the pointer. Rulers are ruler shapes in the annotation document, not yet persisted, and model areas reach the snap only through MEAS-006.
- TILES-008 (malformed capability token) makes verifyCapability answer 401 with reason malformed for bad base64 or an empty part instead of a 500, and a wrong signature now has the reason bad-signature. Edge tests cover all three and check the security headers stay on the 401.
- ANN-001 (annotation document model in the client) adds apps/web/src/annotations, an immutable document with nine shape kinds and groups that keeps columnar model runs apart from editable shapes. promoteRunPiece copies a piece into a polygon with model, version and run as provenance, and deleting it brings the piece back. Shape ids are local until SKEL-007 maps them to server object ids.
- CASES-009 (search API) meets its 100 ms p95 on 100,000 cases: migration 0077 stores lowercase copies of project, case and slide labels and search matches them with LIKE, because row-level security keeps the planner from indexing ILIKE. p95 fell from 48 to 59 ms to 24 to 25 ms over three runs on the Mac, and the test is unchanged.
- NAV-001 (magnification bar) is a folding bar on the live slide, driven by the engine camera, with presets on Alt+1 to Alt+6 through the key registry and a shared blocked-rect function for overlays. Brightness is a CSS stand-in until VIZ-007, and the zoom-percent label exists on the component but the magnification_label setting has no such value yet.
- AIP-003 (tiers Best, Good, Fast) maps tumor, outline and typer to an exact model and version per tier in each workspace (migration 0076, model_tiers), lists it at /api/tenant/model-tiers with hover labels, lets admins set or reset a tier with audit, and lets a model run start by tier and record it, which fills the tier field job progress left empty. The annotate view reads the real route instead of the draft, and Fast stays empty on the server while the web shows the in-browser model.
- WL-017 (filter popover) added Project, Status, Result set and Reviewer chips to the All slides tab with a new kit FilterChip, kept in the URL and applied by the fake slides endpoint, with unit, Storybook and web E2E tests including reload. The Remote chip waits for REMOTE-001.
- AITUM-002 (heatmap layer) colours a probability grid on the CPU with viridis, inferno and turbo tables, leaves cells under the threshold transparent and defaults to viridis, threshold 0.08 and opacity 0.7 from the mock. The model source is data for now, and VIZ-003 and VIZ-007 draw the chip in the layers legend. New goldens cover inferno, turbo and a raised threshold, with 17 unit tests.

## 2026-11-02

- VIEW-027 (outline rendering with casings) draws a near-black casing under every vector overlay and AI proposals as dashes on a white casing, with the mock's four class colours and five goldens regenerated (ADR 0157). On three H&E goldens of CMU-1 a tumor outline misses 3:1 contrast on about 6% of pixels and immune on about 5%, the rest on none. Overlay colour under the brightness dial is left for NAV-003's golden.
- SEC-011 (security headers and CSP) adds a nonce CSP, COOP, COEP credentialless, CORP, Referrer-Policy same-origin, Permissions-Policy and HSTS in the edge Worker, with run_worker_first so assets get them too (ADR 0156). A local Chromium check under the enforced CSP showed module workers, SharedArrayBuffer and WASM threads working. The staging header test waits for the first staging deploy, and each environment needs the UPLOAD_ORIGIN Worker variable.
- CMD-001 (key registry) builds on the STACK-014 decoder with scopes ranked annotate, review, viewer and worklist, then global, key sets read from the settings keymap, a hold time per action and keys per preset (ADR 0155). The whole-slide peek key runs on it, about twelve other window key listeners still move over one screen at a time, and Z keeps the 350 ms hold of ADR 0149.
- INGEST-027 (tissue box that ignores specks) splits the tissue mask into 8-connected regions, drops any region under 0.1% of the tissue cells and writes the box into tissue.json next to the mask. The box is 46.0% of the all-cells box on tumor_009, 89.9% on test_001 and 96.2% on CMU-1.svs, and holds 100% of the annotated tumour on both CAMELYON slides. VIEW-049 should read it from tissue.json.
- CASE-001 (case loader) gathers the project, case, slides, result sets, task and decisions for a slide, with patient fields only in clinical projects, and opens the live slide fitted with a header showing them. The project page row and My work Open now reach the live route, and a dev-only fixture tile server lets the E2E check the fitted time against the viewer.fitted-ms limit.
- INGEST-016 (16-bit big-endian BigTIFF) added a generated 16-bit big-endian BigTIFF fixture in the MxIF layout and proved it opens to exact pixels both by walking the file and from the ingest tile index, with no reader change, because the existing big-endian workaround and the tile index already cover it. Offsets past 4 GB in a big-endian file are still untested.
- DS-008 (tags) was mostly built in the kit, so it added a story of every tag type and tone in both themes, a shared set of allowed tones and heights, and a Playwright scan of every story that fails on a tag outside the set, plus a unit test of the same rule.
- TEST-006 (performance budget suite) moves every performance budget into perf-budgets.json with ADR and result links, replacing SHELL-001's apps/web/bundle-budget.json, and a guard test fails any byte or count limit set more than 20 percent above its baseline (ADR 0154). A browser spec checks time, requests and bytes to the fitted image and to sharp after a jump on CMU-1 at every RTT of the latency proxy. The two viewer time limits are twice the local Apple M4 numbers until the first hosted CI run records a baseline.
- DS-020 (type-to-find token field) added keyboard use: arrows move through matches with aria-activedescendant, Enter picks or invites, Escape closes the list before the modal and Backspace removes the last chip. The list now opens only on typing, a click or the down arrow, not on a modal's automatic first focus, so the model-runs workaround is gone, and the no-match hint finally shows. Ten Playwright tests cover it.
- DS-019 (progress bar, ring and stacked bar) added a tested decision-split helper for the ring and stacked parts, aria-valuetext on the bar, stories for the empty, full, unknown and single-part states and a roles spec, since the kit already had the three meters. The model-runs Run test now closes the Slides list first, because the small modal's first-field focus opens it over the model picker, and DS-020 stops that list opening on automatic focus.
- INGEST-007 (sidecar tile index) makes the ingest index step write a gzipped tile-index.json with every pyramid directory's tile offsets and byte counts, the NDPI restart index and the BIF stitching layout, and the browser reader opens the named directories in parallel, so an 11-directory BigTIFF opens in 0.38 s instead of 1.39 s at 100 ms RTT (ADR 0153). It was merged by hand beside SKEL-006's stored-size levels. Slides ingested earlier still walk the chain.
- DS-021 (undo toast) added a shared undo toaster with a 4 s window and a bottom-edge host mounted once in the web app, wired into guided-review decisions and the jobs-tray cancelled-run Undo, and the kit UndoToast no longer restarts its timer on re-render. A newer action replaces the toast, and the full viewer, annotate, Team and onboarding toasts still use their own. TODO: e2e/model-runs.spec.ts 'Run starts the job' failed on a model-picker click, not yet checked against the base.
- SKEL-006 (open the slide in Viv) opens a slide through a 10-minute capability token, with levels.json supplying geometry and pixel size, and the fitted view matches the Viv golden on SwiftShader with wheel zoom and drag pan. A live route sits beside the mock full viewer, and the format still comes from the original's extension until the slide read model has one. The edge's verifyCapability throws on a malformed token, which went to TILES-007.
- SKEL-008 (stub decision and submit) needed no API change. A new apps/e2e test signs in, decides on a sample AI region, submits, and checks the Slides tab and the task.review-submitted audit row against the local stack, which it migrated from 0051 to 0074. Each run leaves one workspace and sample project behind, and the sample task answers in the 1 to 5 certainty style.
- DS-022 (small modal) now starts focus on the first field and runs the primary on Enter, and an 11-test e2e spec on the built Storybook checks both widths stay fixed with long content, the scrim, focus trap, Esc, blockers and axe. The token field no longer opens an empty list.
- COLLAB-002 (threads API) adds comment threads on a task slide, anchored to an object, an ROI, a point or a shape, with replies, resolve, reopen and a confirmed delete, migration 0075. Blinded reviewers see their own threads and the researcher's, threads on AI objects stay hidden during an open first read, and reply notifications carry project, task and case so mutes catch them. Mentions (COLLAB-006) and realtime thread events are not built.
- INGEST-028 (upload protocol check on R2) ran the upload spike at 1 GiB on parfocal-dev with every check passing, after revising ADR 0116 (resumable uploads) for two R2 doc facts that were wrong. The 1 GiB run made 111 Class A and 3 Class B operations plus 68 CORS preflights, every per-run prefix was deleted and checked empty, and it cost $0. Its spike changes were merged by hand onto INGEST-002's shared MinIO helper. Staging and production still need the CORS and lifecycle tofu apply.
- INGEST-002 (upload UI with resume) done in the parallel run. The Uppy upload engine and case upload drawer have per-file progress, pause, resume, retry after 2, 5 and 15 s then a Retry button, and cancel, and a 1 GiB E2E survived a reload with the SHA-256 matching and no part re-sent. The E2E ran against the spike's Companion stand-in over MinIO, so a run against the real API is left to SKEL-004.
- GUIDE-015 (decision styles with the raw answer and Counts as) UI half done in the parallel run. Guided review, the full viewer and focus view answer and save in the task's style and Counts as, the New review task step and template editor send and edit the decision settings, and the report's Methods block states how decisions were counted. The screen that changes Counts as on an existing task went to GUIDE-018.
- SHELL-001 (routes and code splitting) done in the parallel run. Deep-link tests and a production-build test show the home page loads no viewer or route chunks and stays under 350,000 bytes gzip, the viewer engine is its own preloaded chunk, and the dev-only prototype bar no longer ships. TEST-006 (performance budget suite) should take over apps/web/bundle-budget.json.
- EVAL-002 (evaluation report page) done in the parallel run. The prototype screen got axis titles on the confusion matrix, reasons sorted by size, a name fallback for reviewer columns, and a Playwright spec that renders the seeded report and runs axe on all five scenes. Compare and read-only link stay mock toasts for EVAL-004 and EVAL-005.
- Owner answers applied. ADR 0151 (standing approval within $30 a month) gives one standing yes for additive production writes and sets the whole project budget to $30 a month, ADR 0149 (palette and hotkeys) and ADR 0147 (cloud provider and pilot cost) are accepted, PILOT-005 (usability study) is on hold, and IAM-021 drops the lock card's password field (28a). Fable's priority order and two-agent worktree lanes are in knowledge/agent-workflow.md. Nine review tasks that only waited for green CI were ticked (STACK-014, FOUND-020, TEST-005, TILES-006 and the VIEW reader and GPU tasks), and AITUM-003, RSET-005 and TASK-007 moved to blocked.
- FOUND-023 (task sizes and quick wins on the board) follow-up. Haiku had let real M and L tasks through as S, so four Sonnet agents sized every task that still had only a guess, 447 of them, and every open task now has a written Size line. Quick wins dropped from 206 to 103 and also skip tasks whose title ends in (moved) or (dropped).
- STACK-027 (GPU inference serving) follow-up: ADR 0150 records the owner's rule that inference runs on T4 or L4 class cards (A10G accepted) and never on A100 or H100 class GPUs, with every model fitting 16 or 24 GB, and the spike result now states its T4 is the floor of that class.
- FOUND-023 (task sizes and quick wins on the board) added Size and Needs lines to backlog tasks, a size guess from the Done when sentence, an unblock count down the chain, a Size filter, a quick-wins panel, an unblocks-the-most list and a table of epics that depend on each other. Four Haiku agents reviewed the guesses for the 583 open tasks by milestone and 135 Size lines were written where the guess was wrong, three of them corrected by hand. No Needs: owner line was written because every owner flag Haiku raised runs on dev or staging. MEAS-000 (measurement categories) now checks published AJCC and CAP texts instead of a pathologist, and decision 29 asks how PILOT-005 (usability study) runs without pathologists.
- STACK-027 (GPU inference serving) ran a spike in workers/ml/spike/gpu_serving.py on Modal dev with T4s: a pre-warmed SAM ViT-B embedding container answered in a median 0.26 s with no payload and 0.39 s with an int8 embedding while a whole-slide job ran in its own container, and cold starts took 12 to 16 s with one at 40 s. Surprise: a float16 embedding is exactly 2 MiB and Modal replies over 2 MB cost about 1.1 s more, so AISEG-008 (server embedding service) should keep replies under 2 MB. Cost $0.25 of free credits, billed $0.00, all apps stopped.
- IAM-008 (workspace switcher) follow-up: the query client's MutationCache now hands every failed mutation that its caller does not handle, other than field validation errors, to apps/web/src/shell/error-toasts.tsx, which shows FOUND-019's ErrorToast through describeApiError. The fake API refuses a switch with 403 tenant-suspended when the URL carries suspended=<workspace slug>, and the workspace switcher E2E checks the toast.
- IAM-014 (admin console for users and roles) added GET /api/tenant/members/activity (email, project codes, and last active from the newest session in this workspace) and POST /api/tenant/invitations/{id}/resend, which mints a new link, withdraws the old one and records invitation.resent. The People section in apps/web/src/screens/admin/people.tsx now shows pending invites from the real list with Resend, rolls back a refused role or admin-rights change and shows the server's reason, and the listMemberActivity draft is deleted. The fake seed had Simona both as an active reviewer and as an invitee, which real data cannot produce, so the first pending invite is now ondrej@fnvltava.example. Deactivation is left to IAM-020 (deactivation with hand-over), which defines it, and audit coverage lives in the API tests because the web E2E runs on fake data.
- IAM-021 (lock screen that returns to the same view) wraps every signed-in route in apps/web/src/session/session-lock.tsx, which sends recordActivity at most once a minute, checks getSession after the idle time and shows the kit's LockCard over the inert view on 401 session-locked. Unlock goes to /api/auth/login?reauth=true with the path and query, and the full viewer now keeps its camera in the slide's x, y and z search keys, so the reload lands on the same camera. Surprise: MSW 3 also serves page navigations, so the fake layer answers /api/auth/login and /logout with redirects.
- IAM-008 (workspace switcher) The top bar's workspace button opens the kit WorkspaceSwitcher menu, grouped by organisation, and picking one calls switchWorkspace, goes home when a route has ids from the old workspace, and resets every query so the worklist reloads in the new scope. The fake API keeps the active workspace in packages/api-client/src/fake/workspace-scene.ts and answers an empty worklist, home and project list for the second workspace. Component test packages/ui/e2e/workspace-switcher.spec.ts, E2E apps/web/e2e/workspace-switcher.spec.ts.
- STACK-014 (command palette, drag and drop, hotkeys) kept the hand-written palette, chose Pragmatic drag and drop for later, and built a custom hotkey registry in apps/web/src/keys with chords (1,000 ms wait, my choice), hold versus tap at the Interaction spec's 350 ms and an inInputs flag, shown on the dev route /spikes/hotkeys. Surprises: keys pressed right after the first paint were lost until the hook attached in a layout effect, and the spike's ⌘K also opened the shell's palette because the shell keeps its own listener, so the spike uses ⌥↑ for the in-field binding. ADR 0149 records it and waits for the owner.

- TASK-007 (nudge, reassign and change a task) API half built, moved to review. nudgeReviewer, cancelNudge, reassignSlides, undoReassignment and addTaskReviewer live in parfocal_api.task_changes, and a due date change in changeReviewTask tells reviewers with slides left. A reassign deletes the first reviewer's untouched assignments and sets the new released_at on started ones, so their decisions and first read stay under their name ([ADR 0142](adr/0142-reassign-releases-started-assignments.md), a reassign releases started assignments). The routes took the PROTO-013 (Team) draft names and shapes, so those four drafts are deleted and the Team screen calls the generated client. - COLLAB-001 (realtime infrastructure) built API and edge halves, tests green locally. Rooms are Durable Objects named by the verified token's tenant and room ([ADR 0140](adr/0140-realtime-rooms-and-room-tokens.md), realtime rooms and room tokens). `getRoomAccess` mints five-minute room tokens for slide, task and own user rooms. The edge joins `/rt/{room}` with the token as a WebSocket subprotocol, fans out signed `/rt-internal/publish` calls, closes sockets on `/rt-internal/revoke` through an index in the person's own room, and closes expired sockets from an alarm. `removeProjectMember` and `erasePerson` revoke after commit as background tasks. `RoomClient` in `@parfocal/edge/room-client` refetches on every open, renews in band and stops when the API refuses a token. Tests in `apps/edge/test/rooms.test.ts`, `room-client.test.ts` and `apps/api/tests/test_realtime.py` cover tenant isolation, convergence after reconnect, expiry, removal and the shared token vector. Tested against a fake DurableObjectState under node --test, not Miniflare, to keep workerd out of the lockfile, so the 101 upgrade response is untested until staging. No event kinds are published yet (HOME-007, WL-016 and BOARD-011 add them), and no route removes a workspace membership yet. Nothing deployed, no cloud cost.
- RSET-005 (import checks and fixes) backend built and moved to review, because the in-place display in the upload and project screens is UI and waits for INGEST-022 (per-file problems in the Check step). ADR 0141 (result imports stop for choices after a check): the result-import job tallies unreadable objects by line or feature, objects without a class, classes the project lacks, missing scores and objects outside the slide, stores them in uploads.import_problems and ends, and getUploadStatus answers waiting with needs-choices. New routes getImportProblems, downloadImportProblems (CSV), chooseHowToImport (score default none or one, a class for unclassed objects, class mappings remembered through rememberClassMappings) and leaveResultOut. A file wholly outside its slide fails with result-outside-slide and writes nothing. Heatmaps (png, tif) are accepted, their level and microns per pixel are guessed from the raster size, and a chosen heatmap ends with result-heatmap-not-stored because no heatmap store exists. Six synthetic fixtures under workers/ingest/tests/fixtures/checks plus heatmap-level2.tif, tested in workers/ingest/tests/test_import_checks.py and apps/api/tests/test_import_checks.py. Existing result upload tests now choose before import, since regions.geojson has unreadable features and missing scores. Left: the screens, the share of objects on tissue for each guess, and a task to store heatmaps. Migration adds import_problems and import_choices to uploads.
- AITUM-003 (areas from the tumor map) built and moved to review. workers/ml/src/parfocal_ml/tumor_areas.py finds 8-connected areas at 0.5, lists those whose peak reaches 0.97, sizes them by the hull diameter of their cell corners in mm, and gives the category (over 2 mm macro, over 0.2 mm or over 200 cells micro), a likely range, near-a-line and too-small-for-the-map flags, largest first, with evaluate_areas matching areas to annotated deposits. The range is read from the packaged tumor_areas.toml, still the POC's ±10% with a TODO until MLEVAL-005 (tumor map evaluation) measures it, and the port reproduces the POC on tumor_009, a training slide (7 areas, 8.05 mm against 8.06 mm, result 2026-11-02-tumor-areas-port-on-tumor-009). Left: the CAMELYON16 test slide evaluation, because no tumor map for a test slide exists and making one needs a paid GPU run, so nothing was run in the cloud. tests: workers/ml/tests/test_tumor_areas.py.
- AIP-011 (job progress streaming) done, backend half. Units may carry a small result with an identity seq, model run stages write tile areas with mark_area and listModelRunAreas returns them after a cursor while the run is still running, and a paused run names its waiting stage. Every done step writes a stage_throughput row (model, version, tier, machine, worker, this run's units and seconds), and the listProjectModels estimate now sums a job's stage seconds from it. Tier stays null until AIP-003 (tier mapping), and the slide view, jobs tray and upload window screens are left to their own tasks.
- GUIDE-013 (review order as a background job) done. A task whose scope reads cells now gets its least sure first order from a review-order job on the Postgres job engine, one unit per slide, each slide written with its unit mark in one transaction after a FOR SHARE check that the job is still held. getReviewOrder answers 409 review-order-not-ready until the slide is written and the job's error code after a failure, so task creation no longer refuses missing or unreadable cell files. All-regions tasks still order inline. A rescope cancels the live job and queues a new one with the same seeds. python -m parfocal_ingest runs the workflow by default. ADR 0117 (review order stored per task slide) has an amendment. Also registered listSiteValidations, listProjectModels and the site_validations table in the tenant isolation suite, which failed on main. Left open: no route to retry a failed order job, and the web app does not poll the not-ready answer yet (GUIDE-002, guided review).
- INGEST-028 (upload protocol check on R2) infrastructure step at the owner's yes in chat on 2026-11-02: a targeted tofu apply created the upload CORS rule and the one-day unfinished-upload rule on parfocal-dev only (2 added, 0 changed, 0 destroyed), cost $0. Staging and production were left out because the yes named the dev bucket. Also at the owner's request, all 28 remote fixtures of fixtures.json (26.09 GB: small, formats, viewer, big, huge, camelyon) were uploaded once under fixtures/ in parfocal-dev from the local copies, each checked against its SHA-256 before upload and its size after, in 64 MiB parts, about 25 minutes in all. Storage cost about $0.24 a month (16.1 GB past the free 10 GB), which the owner accepted, about 450 write operations, inside the free tier. The upload protocol check itself and INGEST-029 (shared test slides bucket) reading from there are still open.
- ONB-005 (sample project) done, backend only. Migration 0062 (sample projects) adds projects.sample with a partial unique index, one live sample per workspace. POST /api/projects/sample (createSampleProject, needs the right to create projects, idempotent with 200 for an existing sample) seeds 'Sample · sentinel nodes' with the sentinel node taxonomy, four slides labelled tumor_001, tumor_002, tumor_003 and normal_001, two ready result sets per slide (CAMELYON tumor model v1.0 and PathoSAM + HoVer-NeXt v1.2, three Tumor regions each, placeholder geometry, no cells) and the review task 'Try a review' on the CAMELYON sets with the caller as the only reviewer, reusing createProject, insert_result_set, add_regions, finish and createReviewTask. No CAMELYON16 files exist locally and nothing was uploaded, so the slide rows carry no file and getTileAccess answers slide-file-not-found until ONB-007 (sample slide files), added. DELETE /api/projects/{id} (deleteProject) soft deletes only a sample, with its slides and result sets, archives its task and answers not-a-sample for any other project. Home leaves deleted projects out and leaves sample verdicts out of the week figures through projects.NOT_SAMPLE, and the task evaluation says sample: true. ADR 0139 (sample project flag and soft delete), Proposed. Questions in decisions.md section 20 (what two result sets means, who may delete a project, who may add the sample). tests: test_sample_project.py, the permission matrix gains both operations. test_tenant_isolation fails on main already for listSiteValidations, listProjectModels, getCellSummary and the site_validations table, not touched here.
- PROJ-009 (Can sign out, a project right in clinical projects) adds a nullable sign_out on project members that falls back to Own cases for owners and reviewers and is fixed at Never for viewers, the owner-only setProjectMemberSignOut route with an audit entry and a no-one-signs-out warning, and signs_out_case in apps/api/src/parfocal_api/sign_out.py for REPORT-008 (sign-out of a clinical report) to use. Values are own, any and never to match the clinical report draft contract, which now imports the generated enum. - SET-001 (settings storage and resolution) done. ADR 0138 (settings catalogue in Python, resolved in the browser), Proposed: parfocal_api.settings_catalogue lists 21 settings with kind, default and allowed layers and generates packages/api-client/src/settings/catalogue.generated.ts, migration 0054 (settings) adds personal_settings and workspace_settings under row security, GET and PUT /api/me/setting-layers (getMySettingLayers, named apart from the settings prototype's draft getMySettings) serve and merge personal values, PUT /api/tenant/settings sets workspace values and locks under workspace-settings with an audit entry, and resolveSetting in the API client resolves a locked workspace value, then personal, then device, then an unlocked workspace value, then the default. settings-resolution.test.ts covers all 16 layer combinations for every key, test_settings.py covers the routes. Also fixed: the tenant isolation suite had no entry for getCellSummary from AINUC-004 (summary points API), so its database test failed since that commit. It now seeds a ready one-cell summary set. The whole database suite passes, 1,438 tests. No production touch.
- TILES-007 (access logging for tiles without PHI) built. The edge Worker logs one JSON line `{"tenant", "slide"}` per tile request whose capability token it accepted, through a `TileLog` that `handleTiles` takes, and nothing for refused tokens. The Worker has no database, so it cannot write audit entries per tile. The open is recorded at the token mint, because `getTileAccess` is the one place that already checks the person's grant, knows who they are and runs in a tenant transaction, and the edge never sees the person. A plain record on every mint would log one open per ten-minute renewal, and a client hint such as `refresh=true` could be sent by anyone to skip the audit. So the mint records `slide.opened` on the slide with empty details unless the request carries a `Previous-Tile-Token` header with a token this key signed for the same version, tenant, slide and viewer that expired at most 60 seconds ago. Only someone holding a token from an audited open can skip the entry. The token gained claim `a`, a 12-byte SHA-256 viewer hash of the actor kind and subject, which the edge ignores. `TileAccess` in `@parfocal/viewer-engine` now hands the token it replaces to its mint function. Built as obvious behaviour: the patient's audit trail lists `slide.opened` beside `slide.restricted-image-viewed`. ADR 0137 (capability tokens for slide bytes) gained an Opens and logs row, and threat model rows T2 (token leaks through a log) and T6 (who opened which slide) say built. No migration. Tests: `test_tile_access.py` (10, one open then two renewals record one entry, an expired, forged, other-slide, garbage, wrong-key or other-person previous token records a new one, a refusal records none, the open shows in the patient trail), `apps/edge/test/tiles.test.ts` (three new: ids only across ranges, HEAD and a restricted range, nothing for refused tokens, the console line holds only the two ids), `tile-access.test.ts` in viewer-engine (a two-hour session hands back each previous token). `pnpm -s verify` passed. `test_tenant_isolation.py` fails two coverage checks on routes and tables from other tasks (`getCellSummary`, `listSiteValidations`), not touched here. No cloud service was touched, cost $0.
- OPS-002 (deploy definitions) follow-up from TILES-004: the Worker assets run_worker_first list needs /data/* and /models/* as well as /api/*, /tiles/* and /rt/*, so missing data and model files reach the Worker and get a 404 instead of index.html.
- TILES-004 (correct status codes on data paths): missing files under /tiles/, /data/ and /models/ now answer 404 application/problem+json instead of the app page. The edge Worker routes /data/ and /models/ to a not-found problem and /tiles/ already misses to one in R2. A Vite plugin does the same in dev and preview while still serving files that exist in public/. Tests request a missing zarr.json and a missing model on both. OPS-002 (deploy definitions) must list /data/* and /models/* in run_worker_first next to /api/*, /tiles/* and /rt/*, or the assets single-page-application handling answers them with HTML.
- GUIDE-015 (decision styles with the raw answer and Counts as), backend done. Migration 0053 (decision styles) gives a decision its raw answer, style and an optional picked class, keeps value as the counted three-way value, and gives a task its style, Counts as as one value per certainty step, an optional question and a Counts as change log. The study template presets carry the same decision settings. A task takes the template's preset or its own, certainty by default. A Counts as change after answers exist recounts every certainty answer, drops reasons that no longer fit and undoes a Wrong class retype, and writes the task.counts-as-changed audit event and an entry in the task's counts_as_changes. The style is locked once a decision exists and Counts as after sign-off. Not sure gains Need a second look. The evaluation returns the counted totals and the spread of raw answers. Pick the class blind on cells is refused for now, GUIDE-017 (blind class on cells) follows. Owner questions 19a, 19b and 19c are in decisions.md. Left for UI tasks: the guided review answering in the task style, the New review task decision step and the template editor's Counts as, which still read the draft getDecisionStyle, and the report Methods sentence, which waits for EVAL-009 (arrange one report). test_tenant_isolation's every-GET check already failed on main for getCellSummary.
- JOBS-001 (model run API) done. A run may name a detector or typer without a version, and the API runs and records the latest version from a small code catalogue, parfocal_api.model_catalogue, with the three Parfocal models of the frozen mock, as a stand-in for AIP-002 (model registry). Starting a run opens one result set per slide with source run, the job id and the detector version, and ModelRun.result_set_id names it. GET /api/projects/{project_id}/models (listProjectModels) ships, which retires that draft, and gives minutes per slide, runs ahead and queue minutes. Throughput source: AIP-011 (job progress streaming) is not built, so minutes per slide are measured from existing job_steps and jobs times (mean of the last 20 done runs of that model version, from first step start to job end) once three exist, and before that the mock's minutes per slide stand as a labelled fallback (measured false). The queue ahead counts only the tenant's queued and running runs because of row-level security, and the cross-tenant queue is left to AIP-007 (priority gate). Deferred: lab models and their latency to JOBS-005 (connect a lab model), the finished and paused notifications and closing the result set of a cancelled run to the model worker, and getModelRunDefaults and moveModelRun stay drafts. No migration, no cloud runs.
- OBJ-003 (split, merge and added objects): review_edits gains a split suffix in its key plus retired and merged_into columns (migration 0061), with splitReviewObject, mergeReviewObjects and resolveReviewObject in parfocal_api.reviewed_layer and the pure rules in parfocal_common.object_ids (split_into, merge_keeper) and object_lineage (resolve). Only regions split or merge, the merge outline comes from the client, undoing a split removes its parts and undoing a keeper brings its aliases back, and the reviewed export now writes split parts and aliases. The person mark on added objects was already in place from RSET-007 (reviewed layer) and its test still covers it, and a verdict retype on a retired object is not refused yet.
- MLEVAL-008 (site validation reports) done. POST /api/site-validations takes a model name, version, specimen type, threshold and up to 10,000 items of known outcome and model score, counts true and false positives and negatives, and stores them in the new tenant-isolated table site_validations (migration 0052). Sensitivity and specificity come back with 95% Wilson intervals, and GET lists the newest 50 for AIP-005 (validation mapping per site and specimen). Recording needs workspace settings rights, like announcements, and posts a model announcement that every member sees in GET /api/me/home. A set needs at least one positive and one negative case. No model ran, the tests send seeded scores. Whether the set should instead come from reviewed cases is decisions.md section 18 (site validation sets). No screen yet: a settings form to record a validation and a page listing the reports are left for a UI task.
- CASES-024 (sorting dropped files into cases): Added sortDroppedFiles and applyDroppedFiles in apps/api case_sorting.py with pure case rules in parfocal_common.sorting. A slide's case comes from its folder below the drop root, then a folder or file name starting with a case number such as S26-04812, else it stays loose, duplicates by label, case and size are left out with their results paired to the project slide, and apply creates the new cases in one transaction. Slide rows and result sets cannot exist at drop time, so the wizard sends each upload with the answered case_id and the result-import job creates the sets, and replacing a duplicate is not built.
- AINUC-004 (summary points API) follow-up at the owner's question: the 25 MB is a loading target, not a limit, and nothing refuses a larger summary, which the owner wants kept that way. Measured nine classes, the most a project allows: 27,161,961 bytes with probabilities, 9 % over the target, and 15,640,807 without, added to docs/results/2026-11-02-cell-summary-size.md.
- SKEL-005 (validate and index the slide) done. The ingest workflow already read the pyramid, wrote levels.json and thumbnail.jpg and stored microns per pixel on the slide, so only the test was missing. The upload-to-ready workflow test now asserts the level sizes in levels.json, mpp_x, mpp_y and objective power on the slides row and in levels.json, and that the stored thumbnail's sha256 equals one derived from the fixture by the same preview code. The checksum is derived in the test instead of pinned because JPEG bytes depend on the libjpeg build per platform.
- TASK-012 (a task scope as a list of kinds) done. Migration 0051 (extra scopes) adds review_tasks.extra_scopes, the scope parts after the first, so an old task reads as a list of one. Drafts and changes take extra_scopes, up to three parts of kinds not already used, the task returns scopes with every part, and the review order and its total take the union of all parts with each object once. test_review_order.py covers all regions plus unsure zones in one task (8 objects), an old task's list of one, a refused repeated kind, and adding a random cell part that rebuilds the order and keeps its sample on a repeat. Study template presets still hold one scope, so the sentinel node template cannot preset a second part yet. The fake tasks gained scopes. No production touch.
- TILES-001 (signed access to slide bytes) built. The API signs a ten-minute HMAC capability token at getTileAccess for anyone who reads the slide, naming tenant, slide, original file and the closed byte ranges. The edge Worker gains /tiles/{slide}/{file}, which verifies the token with WebCrypto, derives the R2 key from it and refuses expired, forged and other-slide tokens and every range that overlaps a closed range, the owners included, because label reads must go through the audited API route. Ingest's derive step writes restricted-ranges.json with the image data of every series outside the main pyramid (label, macro, thumbnail, map), DICOM and zip originals closed whole, and a slide without that file gets its whole original closed. TileAccess in viewer-engine renews at half the lifetime and retries once on 401, and a simulated two-hour session with a tile every second never fails. ADR 0137 (capability tokens for slide bytes) records the choices. Found that the Leica SCN header XML holds the barcode, asked as decision 17a (barcodes in file headers). Not deployed: a wrangler config with the R2 binding and TILE_TOKEN_KEY in Cloudflare and Modal are owner steps. No cloud runs, no cost.
- PROJ-002 (privacy modes) done. POST /api/projects/{id}/privacy-mode changes the mode with a reason of 5 to 500 characters, gated by a new permission privacy-mode that only admin rights grant, plus project ownership, and audited as project.privacy-changed with the old mode, the new mode and the reason. A project with patients cannot become de-identified. getCase records case.patient-viewed whenever it returns a patient, which joins the patient's audit trail, while case creation does not. The admin rights duties, the permissions page and the matrix tests now include the new permission. The part about names inside the original file's metadata moved to SEC-028 (identifying text in de-identified originals), because removing them conflicts with decision 10e keeping the original's bytes, and decision 16a asks the owner how. test_privacy_modes.py covers the rest. No production touch.
- AINUC-004 (summary points API) done. GET /api/result-sets/{id}/cell-summary serves the stored gzip summary file, which already holds each cell's centre, bounding-box diagonal as size, class, score, flags, chunk position and probabilities, without outlines. A new ObjectStore.get_stored_bytes reads it without inflating it. Readers of the slide get it, with the AI-hidden rule of get_result_set. Measured: 1,607,244 synthetic jittered cells send 22,636,641 bytes with five classes and probabilities and 15,454,315 without (docs/results/2026-11-02-cell-summary-size.md), and test_cell_summary.py checks the route's Content-Length against 25 MB. The permission matrix world now stores a one-cell summary. No production touch.
- GUIDE-011 (optional and several reasons, reasons on Unsure): verdicts store a sorted set of codes in a new `reasons` array column (migration 0050 turns each old reason into a set of one), Disagree takes zero or more codes of its kind, Unsure takes need_deeper_level, need_ihc, border_unclear and out_of_focus, and Agree refuses any code with 422. The engine counts each code of a disagreeing decision once and retypes when every disagreeing decision has wrong_class with one class. The web reads `reasons` but still sends only the value, because sending codes from the decision card belongs to GUIDE-010 (notes on decisions).
- Backend queue picked by the lead on 2026-11-02, run two at a time in worktrees under ../parfocal-wt, one migration-bearing task per pair: GUIDE-011 (several reasons and reasons on Unsure) with TILES-001 (signed access to slide bytes), GUIDE-015 (decision styles and Counts as) with SKEL-005 (validate and index the slide), TASK-012 (task scope as a list of kinds) with MLEVAL-008 (site validation reports), JOBS-001 (model run API) with OBJ-003 (split, merge and added objects), GUIDE-013 (review order as a background job) with AIP-011 (job progress streaming). Skipped RSET-005 (import checks, touches RSET-009's files), SEC-013 (secrets, needs production writes) and GUIDE-014 (repo-wide rename, runs alone).
- Owner's answers of 2026-11-02, second round, applied. 3a: mutes stay as built, since muted notifications still show in the inbox, and ADR 0119 (inbox mutes) is accepted with disagreements ready getting through a mute. 4c: the drafts were taken as written. 6c: the test slides go once under fixtures/ in parfocal-dev and CI and production read them with a read-only key, which INGEST-029 (shared test slides bucket) now says, with the risk that a dev write key could delete them. EVAL-011 (reader study and outline quality forms) done: the reader study built-in presets the Reader study form (diagnosis, confidence, did the AI change your answer, comment) and the segmentation built-in presets the Outline quality form (usable, where worst, comment), with test_study_templates.py checking both. Existing dev workspaces keep their seeded templates until they edit them.
- RSET-009 (confirm deleting a reviewed result set) done. DELETE /api/result-sets/{id} now answers 200 with the number of review tasks whose slides pin the set and the decisions given on those task slides, refuses with 409 result-set-in-review ('Used by 1 review task and 1 decision') when a task uses it and confirm=true is missing, and writes both counts into the result-set.deleted audit entry. An unreviewed set deletes without confirmation and answers zeros. Tests in test_result_sets.py. The client was regenerated. No production touch.
- AIP-018 (claim cap for failing jobs) follow-up: job_steps has no platform row policy, so the step update inside claim's platform transaction matched nothing. A capped job's running steps are now failed in a transaction under each job's workspace scope. The cap test now crashes the worker mid-step three times, sees the stage running, and checks it is failed after the fourth claim. The docs index links the new summary of ADR 0054 to ADR 0091, and risk R12 (records accepted faster than reviewed) points at it.
- INGEST-028 (upload protocol check on R2) read-only tofu plan run for the owner, no apply: 6 to add, 0 to change, 0 to destroy. It adds the upload CORS rule (PUT from http://localhost:5173 for dev, ETag exposed) and the rule that aborts unfinished multipart uploads under t/ after one day on parfocal-dev, parfocal-staging and parfocal-prod. The plan was shown in chat with a dev-only targeted apply as the suggested first step. Cost /bin/zsh.
- EVAL-011 (reader study and outline quality forms) drafted and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when waits for the owner's answer to decision 4c. The mock (ux-mock TK_FORMS) names the questions and kinds of both forms. The draft adds the reader study's diagnosis options from the CAMELYON sentinel node categories, 1 to 5 scale ends, when each question is asked under GUIDE-016 (first read questions in the opinion form), and one added question, 'Did the AI change your answer?', asked only after the AI. After the answer the forms go into the reader study and segmentation built-ins with API tests.
- GUIDE-016 (first read questions in the opinion form) done. Each opinion question has asked (first-read, aided or both, default both), Opinion gains read (single, first-read or aided), saveOpinion refuses a question of the other read with question-not-in-this-read, and the first read snapshot keeps only its own questions. The default form now asks acceptable, main error and sign as is only after the AI, and the comment in both, so new projects no longer ask 'Is the AI result acceptable?' blind. Forms stored before keep asking everything. Narrowed the task to the API: the form builder has no screen yet, and a task picks the form version instead of overriding questions. test_blinding.py covers both reads, a task without a first read and an old form, and two older blinding tests now answer the comment blind. The client and its fake opinion were regenerated. No production touch.
- AIP-018 (claim cap for failing jobs) done. Migration 0049 (job tries) adds jobs.tries, the claims since the last person's move. claim() first fails every running job whose lease ran out after 3 tries with too-many-claims and marks its running step failed, and retry, resume and cancel set tries back to 0. claims stays the lifetime count shown as attempts. test_model_runs.py covers three dead workers then a failed fourth claim, a retry that claims again and finishes, and a run done on its third claim. The data map gained the column. No production touch.
- Owner's decisions of 2026-11-02 applied. Accepted ADR 0117 (review order), 0118 (blinding), 0120 (study templates), 0121 (S3 client), 0122 (slide row when ready), 0123 (model runs), 0124 (labels, widened so a project reviewer sees labels and macros on their task slides, decision 10b), 0125 (result pairing), 0126 (cell chunks), 0127 (imported cell numbers) and 0128 (streamed result files, now a 2 GiB request with a 4 GiB hard limit, decision 14a). The largest upload is 100 GiB (decision 5b). The cell sample size was already a task setting from 1 to 10,000 (decision 4d), and the five built-in templates stay in code and are seeded per workspace (decision 4a). New tasks: AIP-018 (claim cap for failing jobs), GUIDE-016 (first read questions in the opinion form), TASK-012 (a task scope as a list of kinds), EVAL-011 (reader study and outline quality forms) and INGEST-029 (shared test slides bucket). Mutes stay open as decision 3a, because the owner's note matched no option and mutes are built. The owner's written yes for the R2 dev tofu apply does not count under ADR 0115 (production access), so the plan goes to chat. A one-page summary of ADR 0054 to ADR 0091 is in docs/project/standing-approval-records.md. The data map page said decisions for the verdicts table since GUIDE-014 (say decision across the docs) and failed its currency test, so it was regenerated, and the permission page wording moved into permissions.py instead. No production touch.
- FOUND-022 (remove the mock comparison tooling) added at the owner's request, waiting on PROTO-010 (make the React app the design source), so the shot script's --mock option and the mock instructions are removed once the mock is no longer the design source.
- FOUND-021 (lean agent workflow) screenshot follow-up: a hook lets each agent open at most 5 images (PARFOCAL_IMAGE_LIMIT raises it), and the shot script takes --mock SCREEN/SCENE to shoot the same mock scene in the same theme and print how much the app differs, about 17% on Home. The app ignores the stored theme on load and always starts dark, so the script sets the theme itself.
- FOUND-021 (lean agent workflow) follow-up at the owner's request: dropped the ui-porter agent, so task-runner covers every task, cut docs/README.md from 18.7 KB to 6.1 KB, kept the code and writing style only in the owner's global CLAUDE.md and removed nine stale agent worktrees whose commits were all on main.
- FOUND-021 (lean agent workflow) done after a transcript audit and a second opinion from Fable: board.py gained brief, find, recent, note and tick, a hook blocks whole reads of the backlog and log, AGENTS.md went from 16.5 KB to 6.5 KB with procedures in knowledge/agent-workflow.md, and there are /start, /finish and /consolidate commands, task-runner and ui-porter agents, pnpm verify and a small screenshot compare script ([ADR 0136](adr/0136-lean-agent-workflow.md)). Savings are not measured yet.

## 2026-11-01

- PROTO-019 (viewer tools over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. The six tool screens follow the mock, which draws them all as states of the viewer. Most are search keys on the full viewer route `/projects/$projectId/slides/$slideId`: the seven `navigator` scenes (overview, reading, avoid, travel, cells, focus, pointer) and the two `compare` scenes (picked, picking) join its `scene` key, measuring is `tool=ruler`, focus mode is `focus=true` and the whole-slide peek is `peek=true`. The last three have no scenes in the mock and are viewer states that can combine, so they are their own keys. The navigator's `scene=focus` (the one-cell locator) and focus mode's `focus=true` are different things on purpose. Annotate is its own route, `/projects/$projectId/slides/$slideId/annotate` with `scene` auto or models, because the mock draws it as a second workspace with its own rail and Layers body and because its `models` scene would clash with the Visualize scene `layers/models` on the slide route. `apps/web/test/full-viewer.test.ts` now checks that the case, Visualize, navigator and compare lists share no key and that annotate's `models` would clash. The screens are in `apps/web/src/screens/viewer-tools/` and reuse PROTO-018 (full viewer) and PROTO-007 (guided review): `ViewerFrame`, which now takes rail groups, a tool handler, a Compare slides rail button and an overlay, `SlideStill`, which now draws the mock's 10x image (`look="mid"`) and takes a home camera, `ObjectList`, which now lists measurements, the review top bar, which gained the Annotate workspace option, and the full viewer's Objects tab, toast and keys. The minimap picks its corner with the mock's scoring (`minimap-placement.ts`, the mock's corner score with a bottom right preference, weighted hot spots and blocked boxes), and its viewport follows the camera through the crop centres of the 10x and 40x images. `packages/ui` gained `smart-minimap.tsx` (`SmartMinimap` in its full, travel, compact and 92 px sizes with its map parts, `WholeSlidePeek`, `PeekArea` and `PeekHere`), `viewer-tools.tsx` (`DistanceCue`, `RulerLine`, `ContextBar`, `TierPill`, `TierPicker`, `LayerList`, `PaneHeader`, `PanePicker`, `SlideSlot`, `SideToggles`, `Crosshair`, `FloatingPill`, `FocusCard`, `CellHud`) and `lib/minimap-box.ts`, with 16 stories under Components, Viewer tools. The faded minimap story is excluded from the axe contrast check, because fading is the point, until NAV-006 (smart minimap state machine) hardens it. Two draft queries carry what the API lacks: `listAnnotationLayers` (GET `/api/slides/{slide_id}/annotation-layers`, the layer tree with counts and where new shapes go) and `listModelTiers` (GET `/api/tenant/model-tiers`, the Best, Good and Fast model for each job), seeded in `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario-viewer-tools.ts`. Compare reads the case's slides from `listProjectSlides`. Built the obvious as well: hold Z for the peek and release to return, a tap under 350 ms keeps it open until Z or Esc, a click in the peek closes it and moves there, the minimap fades under the pointer and a click on it opens the peek, `]` starts the travel and a second press skips to T3, the distance cues go to T1 or start the travel, the rail's Measure opens measuring and Remove M1 has Undo, ⌘\ enters and leaves focus mode and the answer keys decide the object there, the two compare panes pan together until Move both together or Shift+L unlinks them, picking the slide that is on the other side swaps the two, Swap sides also swaps the layer ticks, the layer eyes hide layers, Discard and Accept as tumor have Undo and answer Esc and Enter, and the tier picker shows a tier's model on hover or focus. The flow map and the issue states index read the six ids from `apps/web/src/flow-map/built-screens.ts`, `issueTarget` answers them although no issue state points at them, and the prototype index gained 14 links, so with PROTO-008 (disagreements, sign-off and the evaluation report) its count is 231. Tests are `apps/web/test/viewer-tools.test.ts` (7 tests on placement, sizes, the crop mapping and the ruler), two new tests in `apps/web/test/full-viewer.test.ts`, two in `packages/api-client/test/viewer.test.ts` and the Playwright smoke spec `apps/web/e2e/viewer-tools.spec.ts` (every tool link from the prototype index, the minimap leaving the ruler's corner and the travel, the peek from the minimap with Esc and Z, the ruler with Remove and Undo, the tier picker and Accept, the compare picker swap, and focus mode). Where it differs from the mock: the mock-only notes (why the minimap moved, the "was here" ghost, the tool hint bar on measuring and the no-minimap toast) are left out, the 40x stills draw the 50 sampled cells rather than all 929 nuclei, the minimap foot counts decisions from the answers (4 of 5 decided where the mock says 1 of 5), the compare picker lists the seeded case slides (A1, A2 and A3 H&E and A1 CK), and the annotate top bar is the review top bar with a third Annotate option rather than the mock's slimmer bar. Not done: guided review still draws the simple minimap of PROTO-007 inside `SlideStill`, which NAV-006 (smart minimap state machine) should replace with `SmartMinimap`, and the compare layouts 1, Stacked and 4 only switch between one pane, two panes and two stacked panes. `pnpm typecheck`, the `apps/web` and `packages/api-client` unit tests, the `packages/ui` story render and axe test of the new stories and the whole `apps/web` Playwright suite with `CI=1` passed after the rebase onto PROTO-008.
- PROTO-008 (disagreements, sign-off and the evaluation report over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. `/tasks/$taskId/disagreements` is the mock's `disagree` screen with its nine scenes on the `scene` key. The queue, selected, sendadj, sent, lockedqueue, declined and edited scenes are Daniel Hruška's queue of the 7 objects of RT-14 in the global shell: the All, Reviewers differ and Both vs AI filter, rows that can be checked, the evidence of the AI and both reviewers by name over a still of the object, the decision buttons of the task's style with K, X and U, Leave open, Edit in the viewer, a note for the record, and with rows checked the Send to an adjudicator small modal, which cannot pick the task's reviewers. A decision selects the next open row. The locked queue offers Nudge Simona through the Team nudge modal, a declined row offers Send to someone else, and an edited row offers Ask both to look again or Keep the decisions. The adjudicate and adjdone scenes are Anna Kopecká's adjudicator view on the same route without the global shell, through `inGlobalShell` in `apps/web/src/shell/shell-routes.ts`: the review top bar in its adjudicate mode, Reviewer A and Reviewer B with no names, progress dots, the decision buttons and the All 3 decided card. `/tasks/$taskId/sign-off` is the `signoff` screen, the sign-off small modal over the project page's Review tasks tab with blockers and their Go to links, the readiness list, the headline numbers, single sign-on with its failure and password path, the signed version with exports and the change after sign-off, and Start v2 with its reason. The sign-off reuses the clinical report's modals of PROTO-014 (clinical report, sign-out and co-sign): `SignModal`'s single sign-on, failure and password logic moved into a `useSignature` hook that both modals use, and Start v2 is `ReasonModal`. `/tasks/$taskId/evaluation` is the `evaluation` screen in the folded shell: the report as its three pages, the Layout, Download and Compare and share panel, Arrange with the block catalogue, the outline and block settings of the Admin template editor, the PDF preview with page thumbnails, the retired template banner and the failed PDF export. Data comes from the generated `getTaskEvaluation` and `signOffReviewTask`, seeded with the mock's numbers, and eight drafts named for ADJ-007 (queue decisions in the task's style and leaving open), ADJ-008 (send to an adjudicator), ADJ-004 (sign-off as a soft lock), ADJ-005 (changes after sign-off) and EVAL-002 (evaluation report page): `getDisagreementQueue`, `updateQueuedObject`, `sendToAdjudicator`, `getMyAdjudication`, `decideAdjudication`, `getSignOffSummary`, `startEvaluationVersion` and `getEvaluationReport`, with the seed in `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario-adjudication.ts`. The generated `listDisagreements` and `decideDisagreement` are not used, because they carry neither the reviewers' notes, second look, left open and adjudicator state nor a decision in the task's style, which ADR 0133 (queue decisions in the task's style, left open and anonymous adjudicators) adds. The queue's stage is fake server state in `fake/queue-scene.ts` (locked, open, sent, declined, adjudicated or resolved, plus edited, signed and retired), set by `apps/web/src/fake-api/queue-scene-rules.ts`. A disagreements or sign-off link without a scene keeps the current stage, so decisions made in the queue reach sign-off. When the reviews are in, RT-14's task states show all slides submitted, so the project page's Review tasks tab now says Ready to sign off with a Sign off button, and signing turns it to Signed off. `packages/ui` gained `disagreements.tsx` (`QueueRow`, `EvidenceList`, `EvidenceRow`, `QuotedNote`, `ReadinessList`, `ReadinessRow`, `ProgressDots`) and `evaluation-report.tsx` (`ReportSheet`, `ReportLetterhead`, `ReportFacts`, `ReportBlocks`, `ReportBlock`, `MetricTiles`, `ConfusionMatrix`, `CountBars`, `ReportObjectsTable`), with nine stories that pass axe in both themes. `SmallModal` gained `onCancel` and `secondaryAction`, and `PdfPreview` renders real pages, zooms and closes. The three screens are in `built-screens.ts`, so the flow map's Disagreements, Sign-off, Evaluation report and Decide queued objects boxes and 8 more issue state buttons are live. With PROTO-018 (full viewer) landed during the rebase, all 52 buttons of the issue states index are live and `pendingTask` is empty. The prototype index has 19 links for the three screens in place of the one evaluation link, 217 in all. Tests are `packages/api-client/test/adjudication.test.ts` (6 tests on the queue, the lock, sending to Anna anonymously, resolving and signing, a decision next to the reviewers, and the report's numbers) and the Playwright smoke spec `apps/web/e2e/disagreements.spec.ts` (every route and scene with its persona and no page error, the index links, deciding and sending three rows, the adjudicator deciding all three, signing after a failed single sign-on and starting v2, and arranging and previewing the report). After the rebase onto PROTO-018 (full viewer) the full web Playwright suite passed with `CI=1` (133 tests), and so did typecheck, Biome, oxlint, the unit tests and the axe suite of the new and changed stories. One earlier full run failed two guided review tests under load, and they passed alone and in the next full run. Changed beyond the task: the flow map spec now expects the adjudicator's question as the heading of the Decide queued objects box, and `issue-states.test.ts` now checks that no screen is left pending. Not done: the sign-off modal's mock persona is Lenka in the frozen mock, but it says Daniel signed, so the fake view is Daniel Hruška, as the flow map has it. Download, Print, Save as template and the Compare buttons only show a toast.

The flow page stays on the `/admin` route that PROTO-009 (admin, sign-in, sign-up, invite and first-run setup) set up, with the mock's four scenes on the `scene` key: `addmodel` (the lab's server before the test), `addtested` (the test answer follows the contract), `addfail` (the answer is not Parfocal JSON, 38 cells came back without a geometry) and `addmap` (classes and access). The entry point is PROTO-009's own Connect your own model button in the AI models section, and Run a model's Connect your own model link now opens `addmodel` too, as the mock's menu does. No route, shell route id or route tree changed. The page renders in `AdminShell` with the sidebar folded and the kit's `FlowPage`, as the template editor does, where the mock draws the global shell folded with Admin lit. The two look the same while folded, but the sidebar toggle in the breadcrumb is left out, because `SidebarToggle` needs the global shell's context. The screen is `apps/web/src/screens/admin/connect-model.tsx`: Where it runs (address, sign-in as None, Token or Header with a hidden field and a show button, and Read the contract), What it returns, Tiles it gets, and Test connection as the last zone, with the sample tile, its outlines coloured by the matched classes, the answer's numbers and Try another tile. Classes and access has the name and version read from the server, Match its classes with a switch between the Sentinel node and PanNuke class lists, Who can run it with the project checkboxes, and a Summary that follows every choice, including edits made on the first step. Add model posts the draft and goes back to AI models with a toast. Two drafts carry it: `getLabModelConnection` (GET `/api/tenant/models/connection`, the server's settings and the last test answer with its sample tiles and the classes it sends) and the `connectLabModel` mutation (POST `/api/tenant/models`), with handlers in `packages/api-client/src/fake/connect-model-handlers.ts` over the seed in `scenario-connect-model.ts`. What the test answers is fake server state per scene in `fake/connect-model-scene.ts`, set from the scene by `apps/web/src/fake-api/connect-model-rules.ts`, because Test connection and Test again both move to `addtested` in the mock and a cold deep link to `addtested` or `addfail` must show its answer without a click. So the buttons change only the scene and the query refetches. `packages/ui` gained `CodeBlock`, `CodeKey`, `KeyValueList`, `SampleTilePreview` and `ClassMapTable` in `connect-model.tsx`, with four stories under Components, Connect a model that pass axe in both themes. `KeyValueList` repeats the app's `KvList` in `apps/web/src/screens/clinical-report/parts.tsx`, which a DS task can fold into it. Added beyond the mock: the class list switch remaps the classes by name, every class dropdown offers Leave out of results, the bar names the classes still open and asks for a name and a version, and the masked field is labelled Token or Header by the sign-in choice. Tests are `packages/api-client/test/connect-model.test.ts` (4 tests on the untested, passing and failing answers and on adding the model), a rule test in `apps/web/test/onboarding-scenes.test.ts` and the Playwright smoke spec `apps/web/e2e/connect-model.spec.ts` (every scene and the contract, the prototype index, the walk from the test to Add model, Test again and Cancel, and the link from Run a model). The two PROTO-009 tests that expected the placeholder now expect the page. Not done: Test connection does not call an API yet, because the mock's test is a scene and not an action, so JOBS-005 (connect a lab model) must add the real test call.
- PROTO-016 (settings, shortcuts and developer mode over fake data) correction to its entry below. The session policy no longer comes from `scenario-settings.ts`. PROTO-009 (admin and sign-in screens) and PROTO-016 both registered a fake handler for it with different session lengths, 8 hours and 12 hours, and the admin handler loads first, so the settings copy was deleted and `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario-admin.ts` is the only source. Two Playwright specs, the flow map and the upload scenes, failed in the PROTO-016 worktree and passed on `main`, because local runs reuse whatever dev server holds port 5183, which [knowledge/frontend-ui.md](knowledge/frontend-ui.md) now notes.
