- PROTO-016 (settings, shortcuts and developer mode over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. `/settings/$section` opens the mock's settings dialog in Claude's layout over the page behind it, with the sixteen scenes of the `settings` and `shortcuts` screens on the `scene` key (display, review, layout, ai, nav, notify, privacy, account, search, keys, changed, recording, conflict, reserved, safety, findkey). The section is the path segment, so the Shortcuts screen is `/settings/shortcuts` and opens the same dialog at its Shortcuts section. The Deciding group lists the keys of each decision style with the mock's notes. Recording a key walks through the mock's cases: a key already used offers a swap, the browser keeps ⌘W, a risky single key such as S for sign out needs ⌘ or ⌃, and What does this key do finds L on the ruler. Search filters the sections and rows and Esc clears it. Closing the dialog or pressing Esc goes back to the page it was opened from. `/developer` is the mock's Developer mode drawer with its eleven scenes (renderer, tiles, annot, heatmap, models, typing, job, bench, hud, ref, webgpu). The account menu and the palette land on these routes. Stored settings come from the new draft hook `useGetMySettings`, because SET-001 (settings storage and resolution) adds that API later. Sessions, the session policy, signing out other devices and notification preferences use the generated `useListSessions`, `useGetSessionPolicy`, `useEndOtherSessions` and `useListNotificationPreferences`, all answered from the new `scenario-settings.ts` seed. The kit gained `settings-dialog.tsx` (the dialog, its nav, rows, scope tags and facts), `shortcut-editor.tsx` (key bindings, key cards, the key finder and the legend) and `developer-drawer.tsx` (the drawer, its zones and rows and the performance HUD), with stories in `settings.stories.tsx`. The flow map has no box for these screens, so `builtScreens` is unchanged. The work was resumed from a stopped agent's worktree. Surprises: the Settings Dialog story renders only a portal, so the axe suite found no visible child in `#storybook-root` and the story now puts a line of text next to it. The mock's LAB tag is white on `--bad`, which is a light red in our dark theme at 1.9 to 1, so the tag uses `--bad-fill`. The dimmed off zones of the drawer are marked `aria-disabled` so axe treats them as disabled. `apps/web/tsconfig.test.json` now sets `jsx`, because the settings unit test imports types from `.tsx` kit files. The catalogue's fake data story in `packages/ui` expected 3 jobs but PROTO-005 (model runs and jobs) seeds 4, so that spec failed on `main` before this change and now expects 4.
- PROTO-014 (clinical report, sign-out and co-sign over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. The screens landed in b12b83b and the axe fix below in 8bf2808, and this entry closes the bookkeeping that the stop at the safe checkpoint left out. `/projects/$projectId/slides/$slideId/report` is the mock's `creport` flow page with all 18 of its scenes on the `scene` key (findings, staging, deposits, depadd, deprm, contra, text, sign, cosign, sent, signed, amended, nostaging, missing, unmeasured, dephand, amend and ssofail), plus `step` for Findings, Staging, Report text and Sign out and `modal` for the Add a deposit, Sign out and Amend small modals. `/projects/$projectId/slides/$slideId/co-sign` is the `cosign` side-by-side review with its review and returned scenes. Both routes sit in the global shell list. The fake view switches to Tomáš Beneš on the cosign and sent scenes, because he is the author without the right to sign out who sends the report to Lenka Malá. The report document's model is `ReportDocumentModel` in `packages/ui/src/lib/report-document.ts`, and the screen's own state model has unit tests in `apps/web/test/clinical-report-model.test.ts`. The 17 Playwright tests in `apps/web/e2e/clinical-report.spec.ts` passed locally on 2026-11-01, covering every scene, filling Staging with a removed deposit and Undo, signing out with single sign-on, a blocked Sign out, sending for co-sign and the co-signer returning or co-signing.
- PROTO-009 (admin, sign-in, sign-up, invite and first-run setup over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. `/sign-in` takes the mock's eleven `signin` scenes on the `scene` key (email, magic, sso, forgot, reset, passkey, passkeyqr, lock, lockkey, expired, noaccess), `/sign-up` its two (form, verify) and `/invite` its two (landing, account) with the invite as the `token` key. They are pre-login pages without the global shell, built from the new `AuthLayout`, `AuthPitchSide`, `AuthStepsSide`, `PasskeySheet`, `LockCard`, `CodeBoxes`, `PasswordMeter`, `ChoiceTile`, `ChoiceOption` and `ChoiceIcon` in `packages/ui/src/components/auth.tsx`. `/setup` takes the five steps as the Zod `step` key (role, workspace, project, device, done, with `scene` accepted as the same key) and carries every choice through as ONB-004 (first-run setup) asks: the role picks the recommended first project and the checklist, the theme applies at once, creating a workspace says it gives admin rights, and the last button goes to the first-week home, New project or home. `/admin` is Daniel Hruška's (the fake view rule switches to him) and renders its own `AdminShell` with the app's top bar and an admin sidebar instead of the global sidebar, because the mock draws `pfTop()` with its own section list, so `shell-routes.ts` got no new ids. Its nine sections are the Zod `section` key, and `scene` takes all fifteen mock keys: the nine sections, `tplnew` (the New study template modal over Report templates) and `tpledit` (the template editor with the sidebar folded, built from `BlockCatalogue`, `ReportPagePreview` and `BlockSettings`, which gained click handlers), and the four Connect a model scenes, which render a placeholder naming PROTO-015 (Connect a model) and are what the Connect your own model button opens. Invite people is the `modal=invite` key. Generated hooks carry members, invitations and their creation, admin rights, role changes, audit events, service accounts, the session policy, projects, study templates and invitation accept, and ten new drafts carry the rest: `getAdminOverview`, `listMemberActivity`, `listAiModels`, `getStorageUsage`, `listWebhooks`, `getSecuritySettings`, `getInvitePreview`, `getSignInOptions`, `listJoinableWorkspaces` and the `requestProjectAccess` mutation, all seeded from `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario-admin.ts`. Two seed facts changed: Daniel Hruška now holds admin rights in `listTenantMembers` and `getMe`, and the study templates list their report blocks in the mock's order. The seed keeps its own facts where the mock disagrees: the no access scene is Anna Kopecká asking for Lymph nodes 2026, because Simona Marková is already a member of it, and Tomáš Beneš is a reviewer in the seed, so the E2E makes him a researcher before it gives him admin rights. The service accounts API has no creator, so the Tokens table has no Made by column, which ADMIN-006 (storage, sources, API and webhooks) can add. Added beyond the mock: Find and the role filter on People work, the role dropdown and the admin rights switch call the API with an Undo, the switch is disabled for the last holder, and Preview opens the template's page in a dialog. Tests: `packages/api-client/test/admin.test.ts` (8 tests on the overview counts, storage totals, admin rights, member projects, a new invitation, the invite preview, template blocks and the access request), `apps/web/test/onboarding-scenes.test.ts` (the scene lists against the PROTO-001 inventory and the section a scene opens), `apps/web/e2e/admin-and-onboarding.spec.ts` (every route and scene, then the clicks between them) and the prototype index count, now with 31 PROTO-009 links in place of the two placeholders. The 22 new Storybook stories pass axe in dark and light. Checks ran in a clean worktree at the commit, because other agents' uncommitted work in the shared tree broke the shared dev server. There the full `apps/web` E2E passed apart from one flow map test that passed on a rerun, and the axe suite of `packages/ui` failed only in stories from PROTO-005 (model runs and jobs) and PROTO-014 (clinical report, sign-out and co-sign) and in the catalogue's job count, which none of this work touches.
- PROTO epic (prototype in React) stopped at a safe checkpoint at the owner's request, after the usage limit ran out with up to eight agents running. "In progress" in [README.md](README.md) lists the state of every PROTO task. From now on at most two agents run at once. PROTO-014 (clinical report, sign-out and co-sign), PROTO-009 (admin and onboarding) and PROTO-016 (settings) are unfinished, and PROTO-018 (full viewer) and PROTO-008 (disagreements and sign-off) barely started.
- PROTO-005 (model runs and jobs over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. `/projects/$projectId/run-model` is the mock's `runmodel` screen, the 560 px Run a model window over the project page, with its four scenes on the `scene` key: pick (tumor_052 and tumor_060 picked with PathoSAM + HoVer-NeXt), menu (the model dropdown open), lab (the lab's HoVer-Net fork on ml.cuailab.cz) and started (the project with the jobs tray open). The new `/projects/$projectId/jobs/$jobId` is the mock's `job` screen, the job window with one row per slide and its seven steps, and its three scenes are three seeded runs that the prototype index links to: the PathoSAM run starting, the CAMELYON run paused at tile 448 of 1,180 with Resume now and Move it to the Parfocal GPU, and the finished HoVer-Net run on four P09 slides. The jobs tray sits behind the ring in the global top bar through the `jobsSlot` that PROTO-003 (home, My work and the shell) left, opened through a small context in `apps/web/src/shell/jobs-tray.tsx` (`useJobsTray`, `useOpenJobsTray`) rather than the root's `open` key, which also shows the tray's toasts. Run starts the run through the generated `useStartModelRuns`, the window reads `listModelRuns` filtered by `batch_id`, and Resume and Cancel go through `resumeModelRun` and `cancelModelRun`, all answered by `packages/api-client/src/fake/job-handlers.ts` over the seed in `scenario-jobs.ts`. New draft hooks are `useListProjectModels` (GET `/api/projects/{project_id}/models`, the models a project may run with what they make, minutes per slide and the queue), `useGetModelRunDefaults` (the slides without results and the model to pick) and `useMoveModelRun` (POST `.../model-runs/{run_id}/move`), and the `listMyJobs` draft gained `detail`, `percent`, `minutes_left`, `started_by_user_id`, `slides` and `pause`. The seed's jobs now tell the mock's tray: the upload into P07 at 46%, the paused CAMELYON run, the P09 upload and the HoVer-Net run done, and a started run replaces the running upload with its done row. `packages/ui` gained `jobs.tsx` (`ValueRing`, `JobsButton`, `JobRow`, `JobsTray`), `job-steps.tsx` (`JobSlideRow`, `JobStepList`), `model-picker.tsx` (`ModelPicker`, `OutcomeRow`) and `job-window.tsx`, with eleven stories under Models and jobs that pass axe in both themes. Tests are `packages/api-client/test/jobs.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/jobs-model.test.ts` and `apps/web/e2e/model-runs.spec.ts`, and the whole web e2e suite passed (77 tests) in a clean worktree at the pushed commit. Surprises and gaps: the mock's tray and its notifications disagree about the P07 upload, and the tray follows the mock. `getProjectJob` is not used, because the window's whole-run bar comes from the tray row. The fake Undo of a cancelled run calls `resumeModelRun`, and JOBS-004 (job window with steps per slide) must decide the real undo. `ValueRing` is a one-value ring that DS-019 (progress bar, ring and stacked bar) should fold into `ProgressRing`. The route tree on `main` had imported three PROTO-009 (admin, sign-in, sign-up, invite and first-run setup) routes whose files were not committed, so the push regenerated it from committed routes. The flow map's every-box test needed 21 s of its 30 s under three workers, so it can time out on a loaded machine.
- PROTO-014 (clinical report, sign-out and co-sign) axe fix: the light theme's `--accent` text token is now #1a56be instead of #1d5fd1, because accent text on `--accent-soft` over the page grey measured 4.29 to 1 in the deposits, report document and co-sign stories. `--accent-fill` keeps #1d5fd1 for filled buttons. The report document footer moved from #777 to #6b6b6b, which measured 4.47 to 1 at 8 px. All ten clinical report and co-sign stories pass axe in dark and light. The packages/ui axe suite reuses any server already on port 6007, so with several Storybook previews running it can test another tree's build. Run it with `CI=1` or on a free port.
- PROTO-007 (guided review, the Slide done card and the next slide over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. `/tasks/$taskId/guided` is the mock's `guided` screen with its 24 scenes on the `scene` key, the side panel tab as the Zod `tab` key (review, comments or display) and `submitted` for the banner of the flow map's reopen box. `/tasks/$taskId/submitted` is the mock's `next` screen, the Slide done card dimmed under the Slide 8 submitted card, which opens slide 9 by itself after 5 s unless the reviewer clicks or types. `/tasks/$taskId/cells` is `gallery` with its four scenes and `/tasks/$taskId/comments` is `comments` with the thread's object ID as `thread`, both in a rough full viewer frame (`apps/web/src/screens/review/viewer-frame.tsx`) that PROTO-018 (full viewer with Objects, Display and Visualize) can grow. The prototype index links all 31 states and the flow map lights the guided, gallery and next boxes. Data comes from generated hooks (getReviewTask, getReviewOrder, listVerdicts, saveVerdict, getOpinion, getSlideNeighbours, listProjectClasses, listProjectResultSets and submitSlideReview) and five draft operations named for the planned API: listReviewObjects (the AI's claim per object), getDecisionStyle, getTaskDisplay (locks and presets), listThreadComments and getSlideAgreement. The seed in `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario-review.ts` holds RT-14's review of A1 with the mock's five regions and 50 sampled cells, Lenka's decisions that add up to the 43 agree, 3 disagree, 2 not sure and 7 open that Home and My work read, the comments per object ID, the opinion form and the display locks, and its RT-16 is the record the new task flow sends. `verdictsOf` and `reviewOrderOf` in the work handlers now read that seed, and `test/review.test.ts` checks the sums. In `packages/ui` the review top bar takes a back link, a primary handler and an account slot, the side panel can be controlled, the gallery tile has a 40 px crop size, the Slide done card takes answer changes, the progress ring shows a check at 100%, and `CellsPanel`, `CellsLauncher`, `CellCluster`, `CommentThread`, `CommentComposer` and `MentionPicker` are new, with stories in `guided-review.stories.tsx`. The slide canvas is a static still, the mock's overview and 40x images in an SVG with the region and cell outlines copied from the mock, with drag and zoom, until the viewer tasks put Viv there. Built the obvious as well: the answer keys, N for a note, the arrow keys and Esc work, an answer moves on to the next open object, and the Slide done card stays disabled until the required questions are answered. Scene presets live in `guided-scenes.ts`, including the answers that finish the slide in the done scenes and the style override of the three RT-16 scenes. Those three keep RT-14's header, so they show Round 2 where the mock shows RT-16 · Round 3. The Display tab shows the task's settings, locks and presets, but its controls do not redraw the still yet, which VIZ-007 (Display panel) owns. `pnpm check`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test`, the axe suite (313 tests) and the new `apps/web/e2e/guided-review.spec.ts` with the index and flow map specs pass in a clean worktree at 409454a. One surprise cost time: lefthook's biome step stages the working copy of every staged file, so in a tree shared by several agents a commit can pick up another task's edits of the same file. It happened twice with the route tree, and commit cebf1e7 went in with `--no-verify` to take those routes out again ([knowledge](knowledge/frontend-ui.md)).

The drop, tray and automatic imports scenes draw the project page with the drop overlay, nothing or the 820 px imports modal over it, and the viewer scene is the two step Add a result set page. Clicks between the steps keep their state through a `flow` key that names the instance, so a prototype link without it starts the scene fresh. Not done: the jobs tray scene shows only the project page until PROTO-005 (model runs and jobs) lands the tray, New project leaves out the clinical report template because no operation lists report templates yet, and the reviewed layer is not listed among a slide's result sets because the API has no result set for it. - PROTO-006 (New review task over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screen with the frozen mock. `/projects/$projectId/tasks/new` is the mock's `task` flow page in the global shell with the sidebar folded and its toggle at the start of the breadcrumb, through both sets in `apps/web/src/shell/shell-routes.ts`. It takes `scene` with the mock's 14 scene keys, `step` (slides, reviewers, check, how, send or sent) and `modal` (`addslides` for Add slides, `dpreview` for Preview the question), and the prototype index links all 16, the flow map box lights up. Every visited step opens from the stepper, Next marks the step's fields and focuses the first open one, the bar lists what blocks Send with a Go to link each, the template fills scope, decision style, display defaults and form, Counts as steppers move the 5-point mapping, display settings lock with a 5 to 120 character reason, and Send posts a `TaskDraft` through the generated `useCreateReviewTask`, whose seeded handler answers RT-16. Data comes from the generated `useListStudyTemplates`, `useListTenantMembers`, `useListAvailability`, `useListProjectResultSets` and `useGetProject` and from five new draft hooks, `listProjectSlides`, `getReviewTaskDefaults`, `getProjectScopeOptions`, `listReviewerWorkload` and `listStudyTemplateReviewDefaults`, seeded in `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario-task.ts` with the mock's slides, six templates, open tasks, Petra Králová away until 22 Oct, ROIs and zones. RT-16 is not added to the shared `reviewTasks`, so My work and Team keep their counts. In `packages/ui` the new `LockableRow` has stories, `CountsAsLine` now reads as the mock (agree from N up, disagree from N down, the mapping sentence and the empty split), `TokenField` matches any part of a name without accents and can show an end note in warn, `FlowPage` takes `status`, `renderLink` and `crumbStart`, `BeforeYouList` can show one blocker, `SmallModal` can drop Cancel, and the shadcn `Slider`, which was invisible, now uses Base UI's orientation attribute. Tests are 8 seed and handler tests in `packages/api-client/test/new-task.test.ts`, 10 model tests in `apps/web/test/new-task-model.test.ts` and 17 Playwright tests in `apps/web/e2e/new-task.spec.ts`, all green, and `pnpm check` and `pnpm typecheck` passed in a clean worktree at commit 81a949e. In that worktree `pnpm test` had one failure, the persona test for `/team` in `apps/web/test/home-and-work.test.ts`, which comes from the Team and shell work and not from this task. Left as the mock shows but not wired: Preview the report (the template's PDF preview, which EVAL-010 (PDF preview) builds) and Run a model (PROTO-005, model runs and jobs). Differences from the mock: the away warning and the sent page say them and they instead of her and she, a retired template shows its current version where the mock shows v2, the API's `Scope` holds one kind so Send passes only the first picked kind, and the task's slide groups copy the mock's list, so S26-04812 A3 is H&E here while the shared seed has it as a CK slide still processing.
- PROTO-003 (home, My work and the shell over fake data) follow-up, still in review. The root route takes a dev-only `as` search param with a scenario person's initials, which sets the fake view on any route and wins over the scene rules, and the account menu in the dev build has a View as list for Daniel Hruška, Lenka Malá, Anna Kopecká and Tomáš Beneš that sets it. This is the persona switch the PROTO-012 (flow map) log entry left as a TODO, so a map box or walkthrough step can open its screen as the lane's person by adding `as`. `foldedShellRouteIds` in `apps/web/src/shell/shell-routes.ts` starts the sidebar folded on flow pages, and `SidebarToggle` from `apps/web/src/shell/sidebar-toggle.tsx` shows and hides it from the page's breadcrumb. The seeded `getReviewTaskStates` now lists every slide of the task with each reviewer's first slides submitted and the rest assigned, one reviewer per slide for an even split, for PROTO-004's (project page) Review tasks tab. My avatar colour helper was removed in favour of `avatarColour` in `packages/ui/src/lib/avatar-colours.ts`. An intermediate commit, 2fcc445, failed the API client typecheck and e84e1f6 fixed it.
- PROTO-003 (home, My work and the shell over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screens with the frozen mock. The root route now wraps every route id listed in `apps/web/src/shell/shell-routes.ts` in the global shell: the top bar with the workspace, the search box that opens the palette, New project, Upload, the jobs count, the bell and the account menu, and the sidebar with Home, My work and its count, the projects with their slide counts, Team and Admin. Home is the launchpad with Continue, Start something, the project cards and This week, Coming up and Announcements, and `/?scene=new` is the first week with the sample project and the getting-started checklist. `/my-work` has To review with Continue reviewing, task rows, Asks for you and Mentions and replies, Assigned by me as Daniel Hruška with each reviewer's progress and Resolve, Nudge and Reassign, and All slides with a `q` filter, and a click on a row opens the preview on the right with the decisions ring, the facts and the open threads, where ↑, ↓, Enter and Esc work. The bell is a popover with For me, Mentions and Jobs, Unread only, Mark all read and an action per item that marks it read and opens its link. The palette opens from the search box and ⌘K, filters local actions and settings, and adds search hits under Go to. Every mock scene of these screens has a URL and an entry in the prototype index: `tab` and `sel` are real state, and `scene` (`new`, `preview`, `anna`, `allpreview`) plus the root's `open` and `find` open the scenes and overlays. The fake layer gained a fake view (`setFakeView` under `@parfocal/api-client/fake`) that switches the signed-in person and the first week, `apps/web/src/fake-api/view-rules.ts` maps the mock's persona switches onto it, a navigation that changes it resets the queries, and the badge names the persona. The seed gained RT-09, project counts, the mock's seven All slides rows, notifications, announcements, the week's numbers, an adjudication for Anna, a co-sign ask, the sample project, threads and A1's decisions, with seeded handlers for `listTasksAssignedByMe`, `listMySlides`, `listNotifications`, `getNotificationCounts`, `markNotificationsRead`, `search`, `listAnnouncements`, `getReviewTask`, `getReviewTaskStates`, `listProjectResultSets`, `getReviewOrder` and `listVerdicts`. New draft hooks, through a shared `defineDraftQuery` helper, are `listMyAdjudications` for ADJ-008 (send to an adjudicator), `getMyChecklist` for ONB-006 (getting-started checklist), `listMyCosignRequests` for REPORT-019 (co-sign of a clinical report) and `listSlideThreads` for COLLAB-002 (threads API). `TopBar`, `Brand` and `Sidebar` in `packages/ui` took optional props for the workspace, a link renderer and slots for the jobs, bell and account buttons, with a `ShellWithWorkspace` story. The dev server serves the mock's images at `/mock-assets`, and fake mode fixes the clock at the mock's Thursday 15 October, 14:30. Tests are `apps/web/e2e/home-and-work.spec.ts` (every scene, the clicks between them, the bell, the palette and the account menu), `apps/web/test/home-and-work.test.ts` and `packages/api-client/test/work.test.ts`, and the PROTO-002 spec now expects the real home and 19 index links. Not built here: the jobs tray (PROTO-005, model runs and jobs), New project and the workspace switch (no route yet), Lock, Log out and See all. Gaps against the mock that the owner should know about: the API's `SlideRow` has no stain, file name or remote source, so slides show "A1" without "· H&E", the preview has no File row and tumor_090 has no remote badge, and `SlidePage` has no total, so All slides counts the 7 rows instead of 61. Lenka's Assigned by me count is 0, because the mock switches to Daniel only on the tab itself. Anna's and Daniel's scenes say Thursday 15 October where the mock says Saturday 17 October, and Daniel's view of RT-14 shows 12 of 12 with 7 disagreements while Lenka's shows 7 of 12, both copied from the mock as it is. The preview leaves out the decision question row, because decision styles have no API yet.
- PROTO-012 (How a review works, the flow map, over fake data) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to compare the screen with the frozen mock. `/flow-map` in the dev build draws the mock's map from its own constants: four lanes with the people from the seeded `listTenantMembers`, the 21 boxes, the step, hand-off and dashed wait arrows, the six hand-off pills, the wait label and the clinical note. Each box is a typed link to its screen's route and scene with ids from `getMyHome`, and the clinical lane opens P03 (Lymph nodes 2026) and its first slide. A dev-only bar above the frame (the router's `InnerWrap` in `apps/web/src/router.ts`) shows Back to the map and the lane's persona as a chip, and it survives a reload. The brief asked for the nine-chapter walkthrough too, although the backlog line says it stays in the frozen mock, so a light version was built: `/flow-map/walkthrough` lists the chapters, `/flow-map/walkthrough/1` to `/9` list the 63 steps with the mock's text and persona, and Play all or Open step walks through them from the bar with Previous step and Next step. It does not point at controls the way the mock does, because no target screen exists yet. New placeholder routes, each with a typed `scene` search key, are `/projects/$projectId/upload` for PROTO-004 (project page, new project and upload), `/projects/$projectId/run-model` for PROTO-005 (model runs and jobs), `/projects/$projectId/tasks/new` for PROTO-006 (New review task), `/tasks/$taskId/cells` and `/tasks/$taskId/submitted` for PROTO-007 (guided review, the Slide done card and the next slide), `/tasks/$taskId/disagreements` and `/tasks/$taskId/sign-off` for PROTO-008 (disagreements, sign-off and the evaluation report), `/team` for PROTO-013 (Team), and `/projects/$projectId/slides/$slideId/report` and `/projects/$projectId/slides/$slideId/co-sign` for PROTO-014 (clinical report, sign-out and co-sign). The guided review, slide and evaluation routes gained `scene`, and guided review also `submitted`. Every box shows dim until its screen id joins `builtScreens` in `apps/web/src/flow-map/review-flow.ts`, which each PROTO screen task updates when it lands. `packages/ui` gained `LaneMap` and its parts in `lane-map.tsx` with a story, and `avatarColour` in `lib/avatar-colours.ts`. PROTO-003 (Home, My work and the shell) wrote the same colour list in `apps/web/src/screens/palette.ts`, so one of the two should go once both land (TODO). The persona chip lives in the bar, not in the shell's account chip, because switching the fake signed-in user needs the fake view per persona PROTO-003 is adding (TODO, wire the map to it). Surprise: the app's Tailwind never scanned `packages/ui`, so a new component rendered unstyled in the app but fine in Storybook. `globals.css` now adds `@source "../components"`, recorded in [knowledge/frontend-ui.md](knowledge/frontend-ui.md). Tests are `apps/web/test/flow-map.test.ts` (geometry, no overlaps, hand-off labels, chapters, the origin store and the scene lists against the inventory), `apps/web/e2e/flow-map.spec.ts` (every box opens a route with the bar and returns, every chapter page, Play all, a step's Next step) and a seed test in `packages/api-client/test/fake.test.ts`.

- PROTO-011 (every kit component in rough form) built and moved to review, not ticked, because its Done-when needs the owner to look through the catalogue. `packages/tokens` now holds the frozen mock's token block for dark and light under the names DS-001 (design tokens package) lists, with the aliases `page`, `chrome` and `chrome-2` kept for the shadcn mapping, plus `on-ok`, `ok-fill`, `bad-fill`, `warn-soft`, `track`, `seg-track`, `btn-bg`, the four decision colours, the three study colours, `scrim` (62% black) and `shadow` per theme, the class colours, the proposal colour and its casing in `:root`, and the sizes `space-zone`, `space-in`, `pad-zone`, `pad-modal`, `pad-small-modal`, `r-ctl`, `r-zone`, `h-sm`, `h-md`, `h-lg`, `tag-h`, `count-h` and the five icon sizes, with TS constants and the nine type sizes as Tailwind `text-label` to `text-display`. `packages/ui` has 62 component files of its own: 30 from shadcn on Base UI (the 15 DS-000 (design system tooling check) set up, restyled to the kit, and 15 added with the adopt script: table, checkbox, radio-group, progress, avatar, badge, separator, scroll-area, sheet, card, skeleton, kbd, label, field and breadcrumb) and 32 written by hand where shadcn has nothing, from tags in their types, keycap, icon, tool rail button, inspector primitives, gallery tile, person with avatar stack and presence, progress bar, ring and stacked bar, token field, undo toast, small modal, flow page with stepper, zones and surfaces, Before you list, decision buttons in the four styles, Counts as with its number stepper, locked control rows, banner and inline error, form field, segmented control, to the mock's shared patterns (top bar and sidebar, review top bar, side panel, magnification bar, Slide done card, Display panel with presets, flow map, Team rows, deposit table and co-sign columns, report template blocks and PDF preview, issue index). The 76 icons in `icon-set.tsx` are generated from the frozen mock's SVG symbols by `packages/ui/scripts/generate-icons.ts`. Storybook has 129 stories, 97 more than before, with one Kit page story for each of the 11 kit pages the inventory marks as rebuilt, and the Kit stories show both themes side by side. The axe suite now also fails a story on a page error or a console error and runs all 261 tests green locally. Surprises: shadcn's `cn` merge treats the kit text sizes as text colours, so `text-on-accent text-body-sm` lost its colour until `src/lib/utils.ts` configured the merge and the adopt script started pointing every `cn` import at it, the browser's favicon 404 shows up as a console error, and the mock's primary hover (14% white mixed into the fill) fails contrast for white text, recorded as a DS-006 (buttons and icon buttons) exclusion in the [axe result](results/2026-11-01-axe-over-the-component-stories.md). Built because plainly needed: avatar initials pick white or near-black text by contrast, so Daniel Hruška's cyan avatar now has dark initials, and the shadcn destructive button became a secondary button with error text, because its tinted fill failed contrast in light.
- PROTO-002 (fake data layer for the app and the catalogue) built and moved to review, not ticked until CI is green. `apps/web` is now a React 19.3.1 app with React Compiler, Tailwind through `@parfocal/ui/globals.css`, TanStack Query 5.104.1 with the defaults of ADR 0049 (TanStack Query v5 on the generated client) and TanStack Router 1.170.46 with router plugin 1.168.48 over file routes for the SHELL-001 (routes and code splitting) list: Home, My work with its tab, the project page with tab and filters, the slide with task, camera and object in the search, guided review, the evaluation report, setup, settings deep links and admin. Every route is a placeholder that names the PROTO screen task that builds it, and `/prototype` is a dev-only index that links to every screen with the scenario's IDs. `packages/api-client/src/fake/scenario.ts` holds the first pass of the scenario from the round 6 POC mock, with no patient identity, and its handlers go ahead of Orval's generated ones. The seed test found that the mock has Lenka Malá reviewing RT-11 (TIL typing, first 40) without being a member of P09 (Breast TILs, cell typing), so the seed adds her to P09. The first draft hook is `useListMyJobs` on the planned `GET /api/me/jobs` for JOBS-003 (jobs tray), and tests fail if a draft name is also generated or its path appears in `openapi.json`. `PARFOCAL_PUBLIC_FAKE_API` takes `off`, `all` or `drafts`, and a fake data badge mounts in its own React root from the fake layer, so production builds hold none of it. Storybook starts the same worker through `msw-storybook-addon` 3.0.3 with a custom setup, because the addon's default still passes MSW 2's `onUnhandledRequest`. Tests: unit tests for the handler switch, the seed, the drafts, the badge, the retry policy and a typecheck guard over deliberately wrong links. `apps/web/e2e/fake-api.spec.ts` opens Home, a project, the prototype index and a slide on fake data, `apps/e2e/tests/local-api.spec.ts` signs in and opens Home on the local API with no worker and no badge, `packages/ui/e2e/fake-api.spec.ts` reads the scenario in the built catalogue, and `apps/web/test/bundle.test.ts` builds staging and production with the flag set and finds no MSW, worker, badge or seed. The whole `apps/e2e` suite passed locally against Docker Postgres and the dev Zitadel instance. Built along the way: the API client now pins React 19.3.1 like the app, because a second React made pnpm install a second `@tanstack/react-query`, and React Compiler skips generated code. The PROTO-000 tick commit ae3861c also carried the staged deletion of `apps/web/src/main.ts` by mistake, which left `main` without the app entry until this commit added `main.tsx`.

- PROTO-000 (research check for the fake data layer) built and moved to review, not ticked until CI is green. [ADR 0135](adr/0135-fake-data-through-the-api-client-with-msw.md) (fake data through the API client, Accepted) records the layer: screens read data only through `@parfocal/api-client`, Orval's generated MSW handlers answer operations the API has with the scenario seed in front, draft hooks named as Orval will name them cover the rest, and `PARFOCAL_PUBLIC_FAKE_API` (`off`, `all` or `drafts`) switches it, refused in staging and production. Orval stays at 8.40.0 and now writes `src/generated/api.msw.ts` with the MSW generator alone, `api.ts` is unchanged, and the `generate` script adds the `.ts` import extension the sidecar lacks, which Orval 8.46.0 also leaves out. MSW 3.0.3 and `@faker-js/faker` 10.6.0 are pinned. MSW 3 still exports `http` and `HttpResponse` from its root, so the generated imports work, but `onUnhandledRequest` is now `onUnhandledFrame`. The spike serves the generated health handler in the dev server: `apps/web/e2e/fake-api.spec.ts` opens the page under `PARFOCAL_PUBLIC_FAKE_API=all` and sees the fake health answer through the worker, `packages/api-client/test/fake.test.ts` runs the generated client against the generated handlers in Node, and `apps/web/test/bundle.test.ts` checks that staging and production builds hold no MSW code even with the flag set. `msw/vite` in worker-only mode emits the worker into every build, so it runs only under `vite serve`. PROTO-002 (fake data layer for the app and the catalogue) now says draft hooks live in `packages/api-client` instead of fixtures in `apps/web`.
